\displaystyle:=\min\mathopen{\{}i\geq 0\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\neq\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\pm\delta^{-1}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}\text{ for some }\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C},\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}\mathclose{\}},

\displaystyle\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{B}}

\displaystyle:=\min\mathopen{}\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}i\geq 0:&\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\neq\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\pm\delta^{-1/2}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}\\
&\quad\text{for some }\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C},e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I],\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}\end{array}\right\}\mathclose{}.

The stopping time
\tau_{\mathfrak{C}}
is the fourth stopping time mentioned in Section
6
.
Similarly as with the introduction of the stopping time
\tau_{\mathscr{F}}\geq\tau_{\mathfrak{C}}
in Section
7
, the precise definition of
\tau_{\mathfrak{B}}
is not relevant in this section, so we instead work with the stopping time
\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{B}}
that satisfies
\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{B}}\geq\tau_{\mathfrak{B}}
.
We set

\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}:=\tau_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}^{\prime}}\wedge\tau_{\mathfrak{C}}\wedge\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{B}}\geq\tau^{\star}.

We remark that whenever the aforementioned numbers of embeddings are close to their corresponding random trajectories, they are also close to a corresponding deterministic trajectory in the following sense.

Lemma 8.16
.

Let
i\geq 0
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tau_{\mathfrak{C}}\mathclose{\}}
.
Let
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C}
and
\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}
.
Then,
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm\delta^{5})\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}
.

Proof.

Similarly as in the proof of Lemma
8.11
, for every chain
\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}=(F^{\prime},V^{\prime},I)
where
F^{\prime}
has length
\ell^{\prime}
, let

g_{\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}}:=\lvert\mathopen{\{}0\leq\ell^{\prime\prime}\leq\ell^{\prime}-1\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}|\ell^{\prime\prime}}\neq\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}|\ell^{\prime\prime}+1}\mathclose{\}}\rvert.

Suppose that
F=\mathcal{F}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{\ell}
.
We use induction on
\ell
to show that

(8.12)

\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm g_{\mathfrak{c}}\delta^{-1}\zeta)\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}.

By Lemma
8.3
, we have
\lvert V\rvert\leq\varepsilon^{-3}
, hence
\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert\leq\varepsilon^{-3k}
and thus
g_{\mathfrak{c}}\leq\varepsilon^{-3}+\varepsilon^{-3k}
, so this is sufficient.

Let us proceed with the proof by induction.
If
\ell=1
, then
g_{\mathfrak{c}}=1
by Lemma
8.5
and we have
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm\delta^{-1}\zeta)\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}
.
Let
q\geq 2
and suppose that (
8.12
) holds if
\ell\leq q-1
.
Suppose that
\ell=q
.
If
\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}=\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}
, then (
8.12
) follows by induction hypothesis, so we may assume
\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}\neq\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}
and hence
g_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}=g_{\mathfrak{c}}-1
.
Then, by induction hypothesis we have

\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm g_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}\delta^{-1}\zeta)\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},I}=(1\pm(g_{\mathfrak{c}}-1)\delta^{-1}\zeta)\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},I}.

Since
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},I}=\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}
, this yields

\displaystyle\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}

\displaystyle=_{\mathcal{X}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}\pm\delta^{-1}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm(g_{\mathfrak{c}}-1)\delta^{-1}\zeta)\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}\pm\delta^{-1}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}

\displaystyle=(1\pm g_{\mathfrak{c}}\delta^{-1}\zeta)\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I},

which completes the proof.
∎

In this section, we show that the probability that
\tau_{\mathfrak{C}}\leq\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}
is small.
The collection
\mathfrak{C}
is infinite, however, Lemma
8.17
shows that it suffices to consider a collection of chains of size at most
1/\delta
.
By relying on a union bound argument, this allows us to essentially only consider one fixed chain
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C}
.

Lemma 8.17
.

There exists a collection
\mathfrak{C}_{0}\subseteq\mathfrak{C}
with
\lvert\mathfrak{C}_{0}\rvert\leq 1/\delta
such that for all
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C}
, there exists a chain
\mathfrak{c}_{0}=(F_{0},V_{0},I_{0})\in\mathfrak{C}_{0}
such that
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}_{0}},I_{0})
is a copy of
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},I)
while
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}_{0}|\mathord{-}},I_{0})
is a copy of
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}},I)
.

Proof.

Consider the set
\mathscr{T}
of all templates
(\mathcal{A},I)
where
V_{\mathcal{A}}\subseteq\mathopen{\{}1,\ldots,1/\varepsilon^{3}\mathclose{\}}
.
By Lemma
8.3
, for all
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C}
, we may choose a template
\mathcal{T}_{\mathfrak{c}}\in\mathscr{T}
that is a copy of
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},I)
.
Let
\mathscr{T}_{2}:=\mathopen{\{}(\mathcal{T}_{\mathfrak{c}},\mathcal{T}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}})\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\mathfrak{c}\in\mathfrak{C}\mathclose{\}}\subseteq\mathscr{T}^{2}
and for every pair
\mathscr{P}\in\mathscr{T}_{2}
, choose a chain
\mathfrak{c}_{\mathscr{P}}\in\mathfrak{C}
with
\mathscr{P}=(\mathcal{T}_{\mathfrak{c}_{\mathscr{P}}},\mathcal{T}_{\mathfrak{c}_{\mathscr{P}}|\mathord{-}})
.
Then,
\mathopen{\{}\mathfrak{c}_{\mathscr{P}}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\mathscr{P}\in\mathscr{T}_{2}\mathclose{\}}
is a collection as desired.
∎

Observation 8.18
.

Suppose that
\mathfrak{C}_{0}\subseteq\mathfrak{C}
is a collection of chains as in Lemma
8.17
.
For
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C}
and
\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}
, let

\tau_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}:=\min\mathopen{\{}i\geq 0\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\neq\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\pm\delta^{-1}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}\mathclose{\}}.

Then,

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau_{\mathfrak{C}}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}_{\mathfrak{C}}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}\leq\sum_{\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C}_{0},\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}\colon}\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}_{\mathfrak{C}}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}.

Hence, fix
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C}
where
F=\mathcal{F}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{\ell}
and furthermore fix
\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}
.
Note that by Lemma
8.5
, we have
\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]\neq\emptyset
and
\ell\geq 1
.
For
i\geq 0
, let
\xi_{1}(i)
denote the corresponding absolute error appearing in the definition of
\tau_{\mathfrak{C}}
and consider a slightly smaller error term
\xi_{0}(i)
, that is let

\xi_{1}(i):=\delta^{-1}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}\quad\text{and}\quad\xi_{0}(i):=(1-\delta)\xi_{1}(i)

and define the stopping time

\tau:=\min\mathopen{\{}i\geq 0\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\neq\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\pm\xi_{1}\mathclose{\}}.

Our goal is now to show that
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
is typically in the interval
I_{1}(i):=[\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\xi_{1},\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}+\xi_{1}]
as long as other key quantities are as predicted.
More formally, our goal is to show that the probability that
\tau\leq\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}
is sufficiently small.
Define the
“critical”
intervals

I^{-}(i):=[\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\xi_{1},\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\xi_{0}],\quad I^{+}(i):=[\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}+\xi_{0},\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}+\xi_{1}].

As long as
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
is not close to the boundary of
I_{1}
in the sense that
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
is in the interval
I_{0}(i):=[\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\xi_{0},\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}+\xi_{0}]
, within the next few steps
i
, there is no danger that
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
could be outside
I_{1}
provided that we chose
\xi_{1}
to be sufficiently large compared to
\xi_{0}
.
The situation only becomes
“critical”
when
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
is outside
I_{0}
, that is when
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
enters the critical interval
I^{-}
or
I^{+}
.
Exploiting the fact that whenever this is the case, the process exhibits self-correcting behavior in the sense that whenever this is the case, in expectation
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
returns to values close to
\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
, we show that it is unlikely that
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
ever fully crosses one of the critical intervals.
Since, as we formally show later,
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
cannot jump over one of the critical intervals in one step, it suffices to restrict our attention to the behavior of
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
inside the critical intervals.

For
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
, consider the random variable

Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i):={\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\xi_{1}

that measures by how much
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
exceeds the permitted deviation
\xi_{1}
from
\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
.
Our goal is to show that
Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}
is non-positive whenever
i\leq\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}
.
To show that this is the case, for all
i_{0}\geq 0
, we consider an auxiliary random process
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}(i_{0}),Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}(i_{0}+1),\ldots
that follows the evolution of
Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i_{0}),Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i_{0}+1),\ldots
as long as the situation is relevant for our analysis, that is until
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
has left the critical interval
I^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}
or until we are at step
\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}
.
In these cases, that is when
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}
no longer follows
Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}
, we simply define the auxiliary process to remain constant.
Note in particular, that if a deviation of
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
from
\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
beyond
\xi_{1}
caused the auxiliary process to no longer follows
Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}
, then the value of the auxiliary process at step
i^{\star}
indicates this since the relevant value
Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star})
is the last value captured.
Formally, for
i_{0}\geq 0
, we define the stopping time

\tau^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}:=\min\mathopen{\{}i\geq i_{0}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\notin I^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{\}}

that measures when, starting at step
i_{0}
, the random variable
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
is first outside the critical interval
I^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}
.
Note that if
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}(i_{0})\notin I^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}
, then
\tau^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}=i_{0}
.
For
i\geq i_{0}
, let

Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}(i):=Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i_{0}\vee(i\wedge\tau^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\wedge\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star})).

In fact, for our analysis it suffices to consider only the evolution of
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}),Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}+1),\ldots
where

\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}:=\min\mathopen{\{}j\geq 0\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\geq\xi_{0}\text{ for all }j\leq i<\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{\}}\leq\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}

is the last step at which
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
entered the critical interval
I^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}
before step
\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}
.
Indeed, if
\tau\leq\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}
, then, for some
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}+,-\mathclose{\}}
, we have
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\in I^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}
for all
\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\leq i<\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}
, hence
\tau^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}=\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}
and thus
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(i^{\star})=Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star})=Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\tau)>0
.
This reasoning leads to the following observation.

Observation 8.19
.

\mathopen{\{}\tau\leq\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{\}}\subseteq\mathopen{\{}Z^{-}_{\sigma^{-}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{\}}\cup\mathopen{\{}Z^{+}_{\sigma^{+}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{\}}
.

We use Freedman’s inequality for supermartingales below to show that the probabilities of the events on the right in Observation
8.19
are sufficiently small.

Lemma 8.20

(Freedman’s inequality for supermartingales
[
13
]
)
.

Suppose that
X(0),X(1),\ldots
is a supermartingale with respect to a filtration
\mathfrak{X}(0),\mathfrak{X}(1),\ldots
such that
\lvert X(i+1)-X(i)\rvert\leq a
for all
i\geq 0
and
\sum_{i\geq 0}\mathbb{E}\mathopen{[}\lvert X(i+1)-X(i)\rvert\mathrel{|}\mathfrak{X}(i)\mathclose{]}\leq b
.
Then, for all
t>0
,

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}X(i)\geq X(0)+t\text{ for some }i\geq 0\mathclose{]}\leq\exp\bigg\lparen-\frac{t^{2}}{2a(t+b)}\bigg\rparen.

We dedicate Sections
8.3.1
and
8.3.2
to proving that the auxiliary random processes satisfy the conditions that are necessary for an application of Lemma
8.20
.
The application itself is the topic of Section
8.3.3
.

8.3.1.
Trend

Here, we prove that for all
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
and
i_{0}\geq 0
, the expected one-step changes of the process
Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i_{0}),Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i_{0}+1),\ldots
are non-positive.
In Lemma
8.22
, we estimate the one-step changes of the error term that we use in this section.
Then in Lemma
8.24
, we state a precise estimate for the expected one-step change of the random process
X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}(0),X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}(1),\ldots
that measures the deviations from the random trajectory given by
\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}(0),\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}(1),\ldots
.
To obtain this precise estimate, which is the key argument in this section, we crucially rely on Lemma
8.15
and the even more precise control over branching families that we have in step
i
whenever
i<\tau_{\mathfrak{B}}
.
Assuming such control over branching families in our arguments here serves to shift the main arguments based on the exploitation of self-correcting behavior to a slightly different setting, namely from individual chains to families, which turns out to be crucial for our argumentation (see Section
9
).
At the end of this section, we combine the previously collected estimates to conclude that
Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i_{0}),Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i_{0}+1),\ldots
is indeed a supermartingale for all
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
and
i_{0}\geq 0
(see Lemma
8.25
).

Observation 8.21
.

Extend
\hat{p}
and
\xi_{1}
to continuous trajectories defined on the whole interval
[0,i^{\star}+1]
using the same expression as above.
Then, for
x\in[0,i^{\star}+1]
,

\begin{gathered}\xi_{1}^{\prime}(x)=-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert-1-\frac{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\xi_{1}(x)}{n^{k}\hat{p}(x)},\\
\xi_{1}^{\prime\prime}(x)=-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert-1-\frac{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert-2-\frac{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}(k!)^{2}\xi_{1}(x)}{n^{2k}\hat{p}(x)^{2}}.\end{gathered}

Lemma 8.22
.

Let
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i\leq\tau_{\emptyset}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\Delta\xi_{1}=_{\mathcal{X}}-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert-1-\frac{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\xi_{1}}{H}\pm\frac{\zeta^{2}\xi_{1}}{H}.

Proof.

This is a consequence of Taylor’s theorem.
In detail, we argue as follows.

Together with Observation
8.21
and Lemma
8.3
, Lemma
7.10
yields

\Delta\xi_{1}=-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert-1-\frac{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\xi_{1}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}\pm\max_{x\in[i,i+1]}\frac{\xi_{1}(x)}{\delta n^{2k}\hat{p}(x)^{2}}

We investigate the first term and the maximum separately.
Using Lemma
7.7
, we have

-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert-1-\frac{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\xi_{1}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}=_{\mathcal{X}}-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert-1-\frac{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\xi_{1}}{H}.

Furthermore, using Lemma
7.6
, Lemma
7.7
and Lemma
7.9
yields

\max_{x\in[i,i+1]}\frac{\xi_{1}(x)}{\delta n^{2k}\hat{p}(x)^{2}}\leq\frac{\xi_{1}}{\delta n^{2k}\hat{p}(i+1)^{2}}\leq\frac{\xi_{1}}{\delta^{2}n^{2k}\hat{p}^{2}}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\delta^{2}H^{2}}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{\zeta^{2+2\varepsilon^{2}}\xi_{1}}{\delta^{2}H}\leq\frac{\zeta^{2+\varepsilon^{2}}\xi_{1}}{H}.

Thus we obtain the desired expression for
\Delta\xi_{1}
.
∎

Lemma 8.23
.

For all
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
, we have

\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}(i+1)=(1\pm\zeta^{2})\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}.

Proof.

This follows from Lemma
7.12
and Lemma
7.9
.
∎

In the next lemma, we state the expression for the expected one-step change
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\mathclose{]}
that we subsequently use to obtain the desired supermartingale property.
In the proof, ignoring error terms, we essentially argue as follows.
We have

(8.13)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\mathclose{]}-\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta(\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|-,\psi})\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\mathclose{]}-(\Delta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}})\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|-,\psi}-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}(i+1)\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|-,\psi}\mathclose{]}.

Since
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}(i+1)\approx\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}
, this yields

(8.14)

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\mathclose{]}\approx\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\mathclose{]}-(\Delta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}})\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|-,\psi}-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|-,\psi}\mathclose{]}.

Contributions to
\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
come from the loss of edges
\varphi(e)
where
\varphi\in\Phi^{\sim}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
and
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]
.
Note that if
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|-}
, then for this loss of
\varphi(e)
, there is a corresponding contribution to
\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|-,\psi}
.
Otherwise, there is no corresponding contribution to
\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|-,\psi}
, however, we find a corresponding contribution in

(\Delta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}})\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|-,\psi}\approx-\lvert\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}\rvert\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|-,\psi}=-\lvert\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}\rvert\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}.

With this in mind, relying on Lemma
8.15
and Lemma
7.7
, for
f\in\mathcal{F}
, we estimate

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle\approx-\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]}\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}

\displaystyle\approx-\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|-}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|-}[I]}\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})\hat{h}^{*}}-\sum_{e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}}\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})\hat{h}^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}

\displaystyle\approx-\frac{(\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|-}\rvert-1)\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\sum_{e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}}\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}}{k!\,H\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}

and similarly

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\mathclose{]}\approx-\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|-}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|-}[I]}\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|-,\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}\approx-\frac{(\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|-}\rvert-1)\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|-,\psi}.

Combining the previous three estimates with (
8.14
), we obtain

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\mathclose{]}\approx-\frac{(\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}\rvert-1)\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\sum_{e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}}\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\bigg\lparen\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}}{k!\,H\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}-\frac{\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}}{k!\,H}\bigg\rparen.

Let us investigate the innermost sum on the right.
The branchings of
\mathfrak{c}
are two extension transformations away from the chain
\mathfrak{c}|-
that appears in the corresponding contributions.
As our chain tracking only compares chains that are one extension step apart, we introduce the chain
\mathfrak{c}
itself to compare the contributions in the sense that for
e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}
,
f\in\mathcal{F}
and
\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e
, we write

\displaystyle\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}}{k!\,H\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}-\frac{\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}}{k!\,H}

\displaystyle=\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}X_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}}{k!\,H\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}+\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}}{k!\,H}-\frac{\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}}{k!\,H}=\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}}{k!\,H\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}X_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}+\frac{1}{k!\,H}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}

\displaystyle\approx\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{k!\,H\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],I}}X_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}+\frac{1}{k!\,H}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}

Overall, this leads to

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle\approx-\frac{(\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}\rvert-1)\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\sum_{e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{k!\,H\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}

\displaystyle=-\frac{(\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert-1)\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\sum_{e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{k!\,H\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}.

Lemma 8.24
.

Let
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tau_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}^{\prime}}\wedge\tau_{\mathfrak{C}}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{(\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert-1)\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\bigg\lparen\sum_{e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{k!\,H\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\bigg\rparen\pm\delta^{2}\frac{\xi_{1}}{H}.

Proof.

Similarly as in (
8.13
), we have

\Delta X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}=(\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi})-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}(i+1)(\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi})-(\Delta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}})\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}.

By Lemma
7.12
and Lemma
8.23
, this entails

\displaystyle\Delta X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}

\displaystyle=(\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi})-(1\pm\zeta^{2})\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}(\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi})+(1\pm\zeta^{2})\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}\rvert}{H}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}

\displaystyle=(\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi})-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}(\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi})+\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}\rvert}{H}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\pm\zeta^{2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}(\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi})

\displaystyle\mathrel{}\quad\pm\zeta^{3/2}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}}{H}.

Since by Lemma
8.16
we have
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm\delta^{5})\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},I}
, this yields

(8.15)

\Delta X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}=_{\mathcal{X}}(\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi})-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}(\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi})+\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}\rvert}{H}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\pm\zeta^{2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}(\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi})\pm\zeta^{4/3}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{H}.

Using Lemma
8.15
, we obtain

(8.16)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\mathopen{\{}\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}^{\sim}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\varphi(e)\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(i+1)\mathclose{\}}\rvert\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle=_{\mathcal{X}}-\bigg\lparen\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]}\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}\bigg\rparen\pm\zeta^{1+\delta/3}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{H}.

Note that for
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}
,
f\in\mathcal{F}
and
\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e
, we have
\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}|e=\mathfrak{c}|e
and
\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}|[\beta]=\mathfrak{c}|[\beta]
.
Hence, again using Lemma
8.15
, we similarly obtain

(8.17)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}[I]}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\mathopen{\{}\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}^{\sim}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\varphi(e)\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(i+1)\mathclose{\}}\rvert\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle=_{\mathcal{X}}-\bigg\lparen\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}[I]}\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}\bigg\rparen\pm\zeta^{1+\delta/3}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},I}}{H}.

Furthermore, since by Lemma
8.16
we have
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm\delta^{5})\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],I}
,
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm\delta^{5})\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},I}
and
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm\delta^{5})\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|e,I}
, using Lemma
7.7
, for
f\in\mathcal{F}
, this yields

(8.18)

\lvert\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}\mathclose{]}\rvert\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{2\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},I}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}}+\zeta^{1+\delta/3}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},I}}{H}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{3\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},I}}{H}.

From (
8.15
), using (
8.16
) and (
8.17
) as well as the fact that
\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]=(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}[I])\cup(\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}})
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle=_{\mathcal{X}}-\bigg\lparen\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}[I]}\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}\bigg\rparen(\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi})

\displaystyle\mathrel{}\quad-\sum_{e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}}\bigg\lparen\bigg\lparen\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}\bigg\rparen-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle\mathrel{}\quad\pm\zeta^{2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}\mathclose{]}\pm\zeta^{1+\delta/4}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{H}.

Due to (
8.18
), this yields

(8.19)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle=_{\mathcal{X}}-\bigg\lparen\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}[I]}\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}\bigg\rparen(\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi})

\displaystyle\mathrel{}\quad-\sum_{e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus J_{\mathfrak{c}}}\bigg\lparen\bigg\lparen\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}\bigg\rparen-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\bigg\rparen\pm\zeta^{1+\delta/5}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{H}.

We investigate the first two terms of the sum on the right side separately.

First, note that for all
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}[I]
, using Lemma
8.16
and Lemma
7.7
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}

\displaystyle=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm 4\delta^{5})\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\beta},e}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm 5\delta^{5})\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}.

Thus, for the first term, using
X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\xi_{1}
and Lemma
8.3
, we obtain

(8.20)

\displaystyle-\bigg\lparen\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}[I]}\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}\bigg\rparen(\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi})

\displaystyle=_{\mathcal{X}}-(1\pm 5\delta^{5})\frac{(\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}\rvert-1)\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}

\displaystyle=_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{(\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}\rvert-1)\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\pm\delta^{4}\frac{\xi_{1}}{H}.

Let us consider the second term.
For all
e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}
, using the fact that for all
f,f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}
and
\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e
, we have
\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}=\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\beta},e}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta]|-,\psi}=\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f^{\prime}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}
, we obtain

(8.21)

\displaystyle\bigg\lparen\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}\bigg\rparen-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}

\displaystyle=\bigg\lparen\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}X_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}\bigg\rparen+\bigg\lparen\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}\bigg\rparen-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}

\displaystyle=\bigg\lparen\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}X_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}\bigg\rparen+\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f^{\prime}}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}

Note that from Lemma
8.16
together with Lemma
7.7
, for all
f\in\mathcal{F}
and
\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e
, we obtain

(8.22)

\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}X_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm 4\delta^{5})\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|e,I}}X_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm 5\delta^{5})\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{k!\,H\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],I}}X_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}

and that again Lemma
7.7
together with Lemma
8.16
yields

(8.23)

\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f^{\prime}}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm\zeta^{1+\varepsilon^{4}})\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}=_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\pm\zeta^{1+\varepsilon^{5}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{H}.

From (
8.21
), using (
8.22
) and (
8.23
) as well as the fact that
X_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\delta^{-1}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],I}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\bigg\lparen\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}\bigg\rparen-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}

\displaystyle=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm 5\delta^{5})\bigg\lparen\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{k!\,H\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],I}}X_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}\bigg\rparen+\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\pm\zeta^{1+\varepsilon^{5}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{H}

\displaystyle=_{\mathcal{X}}\bigg\lparen\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{k!\,H\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\bigg\rparen+\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\pm\delta^{4}\frac{\xi_{1}}{H}.

Thus, for the second term we have

(8.24)

\displaystyle-\sum_{e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus J_{\mathfrak{c}}}\bigg\lparen\bigg\lparen\sum_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\frac{\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|e,\psi}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|[\beta],\psi}\bigg\rparen-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle=_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\bigg\lparen\sum_{e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{k!\,H\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\bigg\rparen\pm\delta^{3}\frac{\xi_{1}}{H}.

Since
\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert=\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-}}\rvert+\lvert\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}\rvert
, combining (
8.19
) with (
8.20
) and (
8.24
) completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 8.25
.

Let
0\leq i_{0}\leq i
and
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\mathclose{]}\leq 0
.

Proof.

Suppose that
i<i^{\star}
and let
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tau^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\wedge\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}\mathclose{\}}
.
We have
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}^{\mathsf{c}}}0
and
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}
, so it suffices to obtain
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}0
.
From Lemma
8.24
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta({\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi})\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{(\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert-1)\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}({\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi})-\bigg\lparen\sum_{e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{k!\,H\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}({\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi})\bigg\rparen+\delta^{2}\frac{\xi_{1}}{H}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\bigg\lparen(\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert-1)({\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi})-\bigg\lparen\sum_{e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}({\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi})\bigg\rparen-\delta^{2}\xi_{1}\bigg\rparen.

Note that for all
e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}
and
\mathfrak{b}_{1},\mathfrak{b}_{2}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
, we have
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b}_{1},I}=\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b}_{2},I}
, so we may choose
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}^{e}
such that
