\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}^{e}=\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}
for all
\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
.
With Lemma
8.3
, using that
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\geq_{\mathcal{X}}(1-\delta)\xi_{1}
as well as

\Big\lvert\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\Big\rvert\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\delta^{-1/2}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},\psi},

we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta({\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi})\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\bigg\lparen(\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert-1)({\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi})-\frac{1}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}\bigg\lparen\sum_{e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}^{e}}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\bigg\rparen-\delta^{2}\xi_{1}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\bigg\lparen(\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert-1)(1-\delta)\xi_{1}-\frac{1}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}\bigg\lparen\sum_{e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}^{e}}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\delta^{-1/2}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}\bigg\rparen-\delta^{2}\xi_{1}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\bigg\lparen(\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert-1)\xi_{1}-\frac{1}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}\bigg\lparen\sum_{e\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\delta^{-1/2}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}\bigg\rparen-\varepsilon\xi_{1}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle=-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\lparen(\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert-1)\xi_{1}-\delta^{1/2}\lvert\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathclose{\}}\rvert\xi_{1}-\varepsilon\xi_{1}\rparen\leq-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\lparen(\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert-1)\xi_{1}-\varepsilon^{1/2}\xi_{1}\rparen.

Thus, due to Lemma
8.22
, we have

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\bigg\lparen\frac{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\xi_{1}-\varepsilon^{1/3}\xi_{1}\bigg\rparen\leq 0,

which completes the proof.
∎

8.3.2.
Boundedness

Here, we first obtain suitable bounds for the absolute one-step changes of the processes
Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(0),Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(1),\ldots
and
Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i_{0}),Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i_{0}+1),\ldots
(see Lemma
8.27
and Lemma
8.28
) as well as for the expected absolute one-step changes of the second process (see Lemma
8.30
).

Lemma 8.26
.

Let
0\leq i_{0}\leq i\leq i^{\star}
,
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}^{\prime}}\wedge\tau_{\mathfrak{C}}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\lvert\Delta X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\rvert\leq_{\mathcal{X}}n^{\varepsilon^{4}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}(i_{0})}{n\hat{p}(i_{0})^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}.

Proof.

For all
(\mathcal{A},I)\subseteq(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},I)
with
V_{\mathcal{A}}\neq I
, Lemma
8.1
together with Lemma
7.5
implies

\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\geq(n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}})^{\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert}\geq n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}.

Hence, due to Lemma
8.3
, Lemma
7.17
together with Lemma
8.5
implies

\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\rvert\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert\cdot 2k!\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},I}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}\leq n^{\varepsilon^{5}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}\leq n^{\varepsilon^{5}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}(i_{0})}{n\hat{p}(i_{0})^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}.

Similarly, we obtain

\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}\rvert\leq_{\mathcal{X}}n^{\varepsilon^{5}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},I}(i_{0})}{n\hat{p}(i_{0})^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}.

With Lemma
7.12
, Lemma
8.16
and Lemma
8.23
, using Lemma
8.5
, we conclude that

\displaystyle\lvert\Delta X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\rvert+\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}(i+1)\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}\rvert+\lvert\Delta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}\rvert\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}

\displaystyle\leq\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\rvert+2\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}\rvert+2\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}}{H}

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}n^{\varepsilon^{5}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}(i_{0})}{n\hat{p}(i_{0})^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}+2n^{\varepsilon^{5}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},I}(i_{0})}{n\hat{p}(i_{0})^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}+4\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{H}

\displaystyle\leq n^{\varepsilon^{5}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}(i_{0})}{n\hat{p}(i_{0})^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}+2n^{\varepsilon^{5}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}(i_{0})}{n\hat{p}(i_{0})^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}+4\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}(i_{0})}{H(i_{0})}.

With Lemma
7.9
, this completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 8.27
.

Let
0\leq i_{0}\leq i\leq i^{\star}
,
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}^{\prime}}\wedge\tau_{\mathfrak{C}}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\lvert\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\leq n^{\varepsilon^{3}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}(i_{0})}{n\hat{p}(i_{0})^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}.

Proof.

Combining Lemma
8.22
and Lemma
8.26
, using Lemma
8.5
, we obtain

\lvert\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\leq\lvert\Delta X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\rvert+\lvert\Delta\xi_{1}\rvert\leq n^{\varepsilon^{4}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}(i_{0})}{n\hat{p}(i_{0})^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}+\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{H}\leq n^{\varepsilon^{4}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}(i_{0})}{n\hat{p}(i_{0})^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}+\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}(i_{0})}{H(i_{0})}.

With Lemma
7.9
, this completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 8.28
.

Let
0\leq i_{0}\leq i\leq i^{\star}
and
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\rvert\leq n^{\varepsilon^{3}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}(i_{0})}{n\hat{p}(i_{0})^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}.

Proof.

This is an immediate consequence of Lemma
8.27
.
∎

Lemma 8.29
.

Let
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
,
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tau_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}^{\prime}}\wedge\tau_{\mathfrak{C}}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}n^{\varepsilon^{4}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}.

Proof.

With Lemma
7.12
, Lemma
8.16
and Lemma
8.23
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\rvert\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\rvert\mathclose{]}+\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}(i+1)\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}\rvert\mathclose{]}+\lvert\Delta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}\rvert\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\rvert\mathclose{]}+2\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{c}},J_{\mathfrak{c}}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}\rvert\mathclose{]}+2\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{H}

Thus, due to Lemma
7.7
, it suffices to obtain

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}n^{\varepsilon^{5}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}\quad\text{and}\quad\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}n^{\varepsilon^{5}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},I}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}.

To this end, for
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]
, from all subtemplates
(\mathcal{A},I)\subseteq(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},I)
with
e\in\mathcal{A}
, choose
(\mathcal{A}_{e},I)
such that
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A}_{e},I}
is minimal.
Furthermore, for every subtemplate
(\mathcal{A},I)\subseteq(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},I)
, let

\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}^{e}:=\lvert\mathopen{\{}\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}^{\sim}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\varphi(e)\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(i+1)\mathclose{\}}\rvert.

Then, due to Lemma
8.3
, Lemma
7.17
yields

\Phi_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},\psi}^{e}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}2k!\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},I\cup e}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A}_{e},I}},

so we obtain

(8.25)

\displaystyle\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]}\Phi_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},\psi}^{e}=\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathopen{\{}\Phi_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},\psi}^{e}\geq 1\mathclose{\}}}\Phi_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},\psi}^{e}

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}2k!\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},I\cup e}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]}\frac{\mathbbm{1}_{\mathopen{\{}\Phi_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},\psi}^{e}\geq 1\mathclose{\}}}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A}_{e},I}}\leq n^{\varepsilon^{6}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]}\frac{\mathbbm{1}_{\mathopen{\{}\Phi_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},\psi}^{e}\geq 1\mathclose{\}}}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A}_{e},I}}

\displaystyle\leq n^{\varepsilon^{6}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]}\frac{\mathbbm{1}_{\mathopen{\{}\Phi_{\mathcal{A}_{e},\psi}^{e}\geq 1\mathclose{\}}}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A}_{e},I}}\leq n^{\varepsilon^{6}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]}\sum_{\varphi\in\Phi^{\sim}_{\mathcal{A}_{e},\psi}}\frac{\mathbbm{1}_{\mathopen{\{}\varphi(e)\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(i+1)\mathclose{\}}}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A}_{e},I}}.

For all
e\in\mathcal{H}
,
f\in\mathcal{F}
and
\psi^{\prime}\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e
, we have
\Phi_{\mathcal{F},\psi^{\prime}}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm\delta^{-1}\zeta)\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}
.
Furthermore, we have
H^{*}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm\zeta^{1+\varepsilon^{3}})\hat{h}^{*}
.
Thus, using Lemma
7.18
, for all
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]
and
\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathcal{A}_{e},\psi}^{e}
, we obtain

\mathbb{P}_{i}\mathopen{[}\varphi(e)\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(i+1)\mathclose{]}=\frac{d_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}(\varphi(e))}{H^{*}}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{2\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}}{H^{*}}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{4\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}}{\hat{h}^{*}}\leq\frac{n^{\varepsilon^{6}}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}.

Combining this with (
8.25
) yields

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}n^{\varepsilon^{6}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]}\sum_{\varphi\in\Phi^{\sim}_{\mathcal{A}_{e},\psi}}\frac{\mathbb{P}_{i}\mathopen{[}\varphi(e)\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(i+1)\mathclose{]}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A}_{e},I}}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}n^{2\varepsilon^{6}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}\sum_{e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]}\frac{\Phi_{\mathcal{A}_{e},\psi}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A}_{e},I}}.

For all
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]
and
(\mathcal{B},I)\subseteq(\mathcal{A}_{e},I)\subseteq(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},I)
, Lemma
8.1
together with Lemma
7.5
entails

\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{B},I}=(n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{B},I}})^{\lvert V_{\mathcal{B}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert}\geq(n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}})^{\lvert V_{\mathcal{B}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert}\geq 1

and so Lemma
7.15
yields

\Phi_{\mathcal{A}_{e},I}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}2(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A}_{e},I}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A}_{e},I}\leq n^{\varepsilon^{6}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A}_{e},I}.

We conclude that

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}n^{3\varepsilon^{6}}\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]\rvert\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}\leq n^{4\varepsilon^{6}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}.

Similarly, we obtain

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},\psi}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}n^{4\varepsilon^{6}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c}|\mathord{-},I}}{n^{k}\hat{p}},

which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 8.30
.

Let
0\leq i_{0}\leq i^{\star}
and
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,
\sum_{i\geq i_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq n^{\varepsilon^{3}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}(i_{0})
.

Proof.

Suppose that
i_{0}\leq i<i^{\star}
and let
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tau_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}^{\prime}}\wedge\tau_{\mathfrak{C}}\mathclose{\}}
.
We have
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}^{\mathsf{c}}}0
and with Lemma
8.22
, Lemma
8.29
and Lemma
7.7
, using Lemma
8.5
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\rvert\mathclose{]}+\lvert\Delta\xi_{1}\rvert\leq_{\mathcal{X}}n^{\varepsilon^{4}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}+\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{H}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}n^{\varepsilon^{3}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}\leq n^{\varepsilon^{3}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}(i_{0})}{n^{k}\hat{p}(i_{0})}.

Thus,

\sum_{i\geq i_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\rvert\mathclose{]}=\sum_{i_{0}\leq i\leq i^{\star}-1}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq(i^{\star}-i_{0})\frac{n^{\varepsilon^{3}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}(i_{0})}{n^{k}\hat{p}(i_{0})}.

Since

i^{\star}-i_{0}\leq\frac{\vartheta n^{k}}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}-i_{0}=\frac{n^{k}\hat{p}(i_{0})}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}\leq n^{k}\hat{p}(i_{0}),

this completes the proof.
∎

8.3.3.
Supermartingale argument

In this section, we obtain the final ingredient for our application of Lemma
8.20
and subsequently show that the probabilities of the events on the right in Observation
8.19
are indeed small.

In more detail, we first prove Lemma
8.31
that states that for all
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
, at time
i=\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}
where the process
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}(0),\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}(1),\ldots
just left the non-critical interval between the critical intervals, it cannot have jumped over the critical interval
I^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}
.
Then, we combine this insight with the results form the previous two sections to apply Lemma
8.20
in the proof of Lemma
8.32
.

Lemma 8.31
.

Let
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})\leq-\delta^{2}\xi_{1}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})
.

Proof.

Together with Lemma
8.1
, Lemma
7.4
implies
\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}\geq 1
and
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}(0)<\xi_{0}(0)
, so we have
\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\geq 1
.
Thus, by definition of
\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}
, for
i:=\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}-1
, we have
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}\leq\xi_{0}
and thus

Z_{i}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}={\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}-\xi_{1}\leq-\delta\xi_{1}.

Furthermore, since
\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\leq\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}^{\prime}}\wedge\tau_{\mathfrak{C}}
, we may apply Lemma
8.27
to obtain

Z_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})=Z_{i}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}+\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\leq Z_{i}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}+\delta^{2}\xi_{1}\leq-\delta\xi_{1}+\delta^{2}\xi_{1}\leq-\delta^{2}\xi_{1}.

Since Lemma
8.5
entails
\Delta\xi_{1}\leq 0
, this completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 8.32
.

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau_{\mathfrak{C}}\leq\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-n^{\varepsilon^{3}})
.

Proof.

Considering Observation
8.18
, it suffices to show that

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau\leq\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{C}}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-n^{2\varepsilon^{3}}).

Hence, by Observation
8.19
, is suffices to show that for
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
, we have

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-n^{3\varepsilon^{3}}).

Due to Lemma
8.31
, we have

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{]}\leq\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i^{\star})-Z_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})>\delta^{2}\xi_{1}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})\mathclose{]}\leq\sum_{0\leq i\leq i^{\star}}\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z_{i}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i^{\star})-Z_{i}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}>\delta^{2}\xi_{1}\mathclose{]}.

Thus, for
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
, it suffices to obtain

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z_{i}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i^{\star})-Z_{i}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}>\delta^{2}\xi_{1}\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-n^{4\varepsilon^{3}}).

We show that this bound is a consequence of We show that this bound is a consequence of Freedman’s inequality for supermartingales.

Let us turn to the details.
Lemma
8.25
shows that
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(i),Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(i+1),\ldots
is a supermartingale, while Lemma
8.28
provides the bound
\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(j)\rvert\leq n^{\varepsilon^{3}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}/(n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}})
for all
j\geq i
and Lemma
8.30
provides the bound
\sum_{j\geq i}\mathbb{E}_{j}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(j)\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq n^{\varepsilon^{3}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}
.
Hence, we may apply Lemma
8.20
to obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z_{i}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i^{\star})-Z_{i}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}>\delta^{2}\xi_{1}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle\leq\exp\bigg\lparen-\frac{\delta^{4}\xi_{1}^{2}}{2n^{\varepsilon^{3}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}}{n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}(\delta^{2}\xi_{1}+n^{\varepsilon^{3}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I})}\bigg\rparen\leq\exp\bigg\lparen-\frac{\delta^{4}\xi_{1}^{2}n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}{4n^{2\varepsilon^{3}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{c},I}^{2}}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle=\exp\bigg\lparen-\frac{\delta^{2}n^{2\varepsilon^{2}}}{4n^{2\varepsilon^{3}}}\bigg\rparen\leq\exp(-n^{4\varepsilon^{3}}),

which completes the proof.
∎

9.
Branching families

This section is dedicated to introducing and analyzing the special setup based on branching families that we rely on for exploiting the self-correcting behavior of the process.
Suppose that
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
, consider a chain
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C}
and
\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}
.
As suggested by our definition of
\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{B}}
, we wish to show that
\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\Phi_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}},\psi}
is typically close to
\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}
, however, instead of choosing
\delta^{-1/2}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}},I}
as the error term that quantifies the deviation that we allow, we use
\varepsilon^{-\chi_{\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}},I}
for a carefully chosen
error parameter

\chi_{\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}
that crucially depends on the branching family
\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
.

Considering branching families instead of individual chains and using different error terms for different branching families allows us to overcome the following obstacles that we encounter when attempting to exploit self-correcting behavior.
When we analyze the expected one-step changes of
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
for a chain
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C}
and
\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}
using Lemma
8.15
, different chains besides
\mathfrak{c}
itself play an important role and their behavior could undermine the self-correcting drift that would naturally steer
\Phi_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}
closer to the anticipated trajectory whenever it deviates.
In an attempt to control this we might want to allow only significantly smaller deviations for these other chains such that the self-correcting drift still dominates.
This approach leads to the desire to implement a hierarchy of error terms such that the error terms of other chains that appear as transformations of
\mathfrak{c}
are negligible.
If
\mathcal{F}
is not symmetric, on the level of individual chains, necessary negligibility may form cyclic dependencies that make it impossible to find such a hierarchy.
However, since relevant other chains that appear as transformations always appear in groups, analyzing these groups instead allows us to reduce the aforementioned directed cyclic structures to loops such that on the level of branching families, such a hierarchic approach is feasible.

In Section
9.1
, we discuss the careful choice of error parameters.
In Section
9.2
, we subsequently employ supermartingale concentration techniques that exploit the self-correcting behavior to show that branching families typically behave as expected such that our dependence on the stopping time
\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{B}}
in Section
8.3
is justified.

9.1.
Error parameter

This section is dedicated to providing and analyzing appropriate choices for the error parameters mentioned in the beginning of Section
9
.
To this end, we introduce the following concepts.
For a sequence
F=\mathcal{F}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{\ell}
of copies of
\mathcal{F}
, we define

\chi_{F}:=-\varepsilon^{-5k(k+1)}\sum_{1\leq i\leq\ell-1}\varepsilon^{5k\lvert V_{\mathcal{F}_{i}}\cap V_{\mathcal{F}_{i+1}}\rvert}.

For a chain
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)
, we say that a subsequence
F^{\prime}=\mathcal{F}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{\ell}
of
F
is
\mathfrak{c}
-sufficient
if
(\mathcal{F}_{1}+\ldots+\mathcal{F}_{\ell})[V]=\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}
and we say that
F^{\prime}
is
minimally
\mathfrak{c}
-sufficient
if
F^{\prime}
is
\mathfrak{c}
-sufficient while no proper subsequence of
F^{\prime}
is
\mathfrak{c}
-sufficient.
The
error parameter
of
\mathfrak{c}
is

\chi_{\mathfrak{c}}:=\lvert V\rvert+\min_{F^{\prime}\colon\text{$F^{\prime}$ is minimally\penalty\ $\mathfrak{c}$-sufficient}}\chi_{F^{\prime}}.

We observe that for all
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]
, all error parameters of branchings
\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
are equal (see Lemma
9.2
), which we obtain as a consequence of the following observation.

Observation 9.1
.

Suppose that
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)
is a chain and suppose that
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]
.
Let
\mathfrak{b},\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
.
Suppose that
\mathcal{F}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{\ell}
is
\mathfrak{b}
-sufficient and that
\mathcal{F}_{\ell}^{\prime}
is the last element in the first component of
\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}
.
Then,
\mathcal{F}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{\ell-1},\mathcal{F}_{\ell}^{\prime}
is
\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}
sufficient.

Lemma 9.2
.

Suppose that
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)
is a chain and suppose that
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]
.
Let
\mathfrak{b},\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
.
Then,
\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}=\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}
.

Proof.

Suppose that
F=\mathcal{F}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{\ell}
is minimally
\mathfrak{b}
-sufficient.
Due to symmetry, it suffices to show that there exists a minimally
\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}
-sufficient sequence
F^{\prime}
with
\chi_{F^{\prime}}=\chi_{F}
.
Suppose that
\mathcal{F}^{\prime}
is the last element in the first component of
\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}
and let
F^{\prime}:=\mathcal{F}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{\ell-1},\mathcal{F}^{\prime}
.
By Observation
9.1
, the sequence
F^{\prime}
is
\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}
-sufficient.
Furthermore, for every
\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}
-sufficient subsequence of
F^{\prime}
, replacing the last element with
\mathcal{F}_{\ell}
yields a subsequence of
F
which again by Lemma
9.1
is
\mathfrak{b}
-sufficient.
Hence, since
F
is minimally
\mathfrak{b}
-sufficient, the sequence
F^{\prime}
is minimally
\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}
-sufficient.
Furthermore, we have

V_{\mathcal{F}_{\ell-1}}\cap V_{\mathcal{F}_{\ell}}=V_{\mathcal{F}_{\ell-1}}\cap e=V_{\mathcal{F}_{\ell-1}}\cap V_{\mathcal{F}^{\prime}}

and thus
\chi_{F^{\prime}}=\chi_{F}
.
∎

For a chain
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)
and
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]
, this allows us to choose the error parameter
\chi_{\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}
of
\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
such that
\chi_{\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}=\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}
for all
\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
.
The key property of our error parameters that we formally state in Lemma
9.8
is that whenever we consider the branching
\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}
of a branching
\mathfrak{b}
of a chain
\mathfrak{c}\in\mathfrak{C}
, then
\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}\leq\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}-1
or we are in a situation where the branching families of
\mathfrak{c}
and
\mathfrak{b}
are essentially the same.

To formally state the close relationship between branching families that we encounter whenever the branching of a branching has the same error parameter, we introduce the following term.
For two chains
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)
and
\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}=(F^{\prime},V^{\prime},I^{\prime})
and edges
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]
and
e^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}}[I^{\prime}]
, we say that the branching families
\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
and
\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}}^{e^{\prime}}
are
template equivalent
if there exists a bijection
\gamma\colon\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}}^{e^{\prime}}
such that for all
\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
, the chain template
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}},I)
is a copy of
(\mathcal{C}_{\gamma(\mathfrak{b})},I^{\prime})
while
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}|\mathord{-}},I)
is a copy of
(\mathcal{C}_{\gamma(\mathfrak{b})|\mathord{-}},I^{\prime})
.
We encounter such a close relationship between branching families for example when comparing the branching family of a chain and the branching family of the corresponding support (see Lemma
9.3
).

To show that we have template equivalence of relevant branching families, we argue based on a refined notion of copy for templates.
More specifically, for two templates
(\mathcal{A},I)
and
(\mathcal{B},J)
and
a\in\mathcal{A}
and
b\in\mathcal{B}
, we say that
(\mathcal{B},J)
is a copy of
(\mathcal{A},I)

with
b
playing the role of
a
if there exists a bijection
\varphi\colon V_{\mathcal{A}}\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}V_{\mathcal{B}}
with
\varphi(e)\in\mathcal{B}
for all
e\in\mathcal{A}
,
\varphi^{-1}(e)\in\mathcal{A}
for all
e\in\mathcal{B}
,
\varphi(I)=J
and
\varphi(a)=b
.
Lemma
9.4
states the connection between this notion of copy and template equivalence that we rely on.

Lemmas
9.5
–
9.7
serve as further preparation for the proof of Lemma
9.8
.

Lemma 9.3
.

Suppose that
\mathfrak{s}
is the
e
-support of a chain
\mathfrak{c}
.
Then,
\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
and
\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{s}}^{e}
are template equivalent.

Proof.

Suppose that
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)
where
F
has length
\ell
.
Let
\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e
where
f\in\mathcal{F}
.
We have
\mathfrak{s}=\mathfrak{c}|\beta|\text{r}|\mathord{-}
, so the chain template given by
\mathfrak{s}|\beta
is a copy of the chain template given by
\mathfrak{c}|\beta|\text{r}
.
Since
\mathfrak{s}
is the
e
-support of
\mathfrak{c}
, we have
\mathfrak{s}|\beta|\text{r}=\mathfrak{s}|\beta
.
Thus, the chain template given by
\mathfrak{s}|\beta|\text{r}
is a copy of the chain template given by
\mathfrak{c}|\beta|\text{r}
.
Furthermore, we additionally have
\mathfrak{c}|\beta|\text{r}|\mathord{-}=\mathfrak{s}=\mathfrak{s}|\beta|\text{r}|\mathord{-}
so a bijection
\gamma\colon\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{s}}^{e}
as in the definition of template equivalence exists.
∎

Lemma 9.4
.

Suppose that
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)
is the
e
-support of a chain.
Suppose that
\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}=(F^{\prime},V^{\prime},I^{\prime})
is a chain such that for some
e^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}}[I^{\prime}]
, the template
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}},I^{\prime})
is a copy of
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},I)
with
e^{\prime}
playing the role of
e
.
Then,
\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
and
\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}}^{e^{\prime}}
are template equivalent.

Proof.

Suppose that
\varphi\colon V\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}V^{\prime}
is a bijection with
\varphi(e)\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}}
for all
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}
and
\varphi^{-1}(e)\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}}
for all
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}
,
\varphi(I)=I^{\prime}
and
\varphi(e)=e^{\prime}
.
Suppose that
\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
where
\mathfrak{b}=\mathfrak{c}|\beta|\text{r}
for some
\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e
where
f\in\mathcal{F}
.
Let
\beta^{\prime}:=\varphi\circ\beta
and
\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}:=\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}|\beta^{\prime}|\text{r}
.
To see that assigning
\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}
as the image of
\mathfrak{b}
under a map
\gamma\colon\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}\rightarrow\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}}^{e^{\prime}}
yields a bijection as desired, it suffices to show that
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}},I)
is a copy of
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}},I^{\prime})
while
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}|\mathord{-}},I)
is a copy of
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}|\mathord{-}},I^{\prime})
.

First, observe that there exists a bijection

\varphi_{+}\colon V\cup V_{\mathcal{F}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\beta}}\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}V^{\prime}\cup V_{\mathcal{F}_{\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}}^{\beta^{\prime}}}

with
\varphi_{+}|_{V}=\varphi
such that
\varphi_{+}(e)\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}
for all
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}}
and
\varphi_{+}^{-1}(e)\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}
for all
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}}
.
Hence,
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}|\beta},I)
is a copy of
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}|\beta^{\prime}},I^{\prime})
.
Since
\mathfrak{c}
is the
e
-support of a chain, we have
\mathfrak{c}|\beta|\text{r}=\mathfrak{c}|\beta
and thus
\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}|\beta^{\prime}|\text{r}=\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}|\beta^{\prime}
; so
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}},I)
is a copy of
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}},I^{\prime})
.
Furthermore, we obtain
\mathfrak{b}|\mathord{-}=\mathfrak{c}
and
\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}|\mathord{-}=\mathfrak{c}^{\prime}
, which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 9.5
.

Suppose that
\mathfrak{c}=(I,F,V)
is a chain with
F=\mathcal{F}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{\ell}
.
For
1\leq i\leq\ell
, let
V_{i}:=V_{\mathcal{F}_{i}}
.
Let
1\leq i\leq i^{\prime}\leq j^{\prime}\leq j\leq\ell
.
Then,
V_{i}\cap V_{j}\subseteq V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{j^{\prime}}
.

Proof.

Since
F
is a vertex-separated loose path, we have
V_{i}\cap V_{j}\subseteq V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{j}
and
V_{i}\cap V_{j}\subseteq V_{j^{\prime}}\cap V_{j}
.
Thus,

V_{i}\cap V_{j}\subseteq V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{j^{\prime}}\cap V_{j}\subseteq V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{j^{\prime}},

which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 9.6
.

Suppose that
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C}
is a chain and that
\mathcal{F}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{\ell}
is minimally
\mathfrak{c}
-sufficient.
For
1\leq i\leq\ell
, let
V_{i}:=V_{\mathcal{F}_{i}}
.
Then, for
1\leq i\leq j\leq\ell
where
i\leq j-2
, we have

\lvert V_{i}\cap V_{j}\rvert\leq\min_{i\leq i^{\prime}\leq j-1}\lvert V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}\rvert-1.

Proof.

Let
i+1\leq i^{\prime\prime}\leq j-1
such that

\min_{i\leq i^{\prime}\leq j-1}\lvert V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}\rvert=\min_{i^{\prime\prime}-1\leq i^{\prime}\leq i^{\prime\prime}}\lvert V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}\rvert.

Then, since Lemma
9.5
entails
\lvert V_{i}\cap V_{j}\rvert\leq\lvert V_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}\rvert
, it suffices to show that

\lvert V_{i^{\prime\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime\prime}+1}\rvert\leq\min_{i^{\prime\prime}-1\leq i^{\prime}\leq i^{\prime\prime}}\lvert V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}\rvert-1.

To prove this, we use contraposition and argue as follows.
Suppose now that
\mathfrak{c}=(F_{0},V,I)
is a chain and that
F=\mathcal{F}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{\ell}
is minimally
\mathfrak{c}
-sufficient.
Let
\mathcal{C}:=\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}
.
For
1\leq i\leq\ell
, let
V_{i}:=V_{\mathcal{F}_{i}}
.
Suppose that there exists
2\leq i^{\prime}\leq\ell-1
with

\lvert V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}\rvert\geq\lvert V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}}\rvert\quad\text{or}\quad\lvert V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}\rvert\geq\lvert V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}\rvert.

We show that then, for

U:=\Big\lparen\bigcup_{1\leq i\leq\ell\colon i\neq i^{\prime}}V_{i}\Big\rparen\cap V,\quad J:=U\cap V_{i^{\prime}},

we have
U\neq V
and that furthermore, as a consequence of Lemma
8.9
, we have

\rho_{\mathcal{C},U}=\rho_{\mathcal{F}_{i^{\prime}}[V_{i}\cap V],J}\leq\rho_{\mathcal{F}}.

This implies that
W_{\mathfrak{c}}\neq V
and hence
\mathfrak{c}\neq\mathfrak{c}|\text{r}
.
With Lemma
8.4
, this yields
\mathfrak{c}\notin\mathfrak{C}
and thus completes the proof by contraposition.

Let us turn to the details.
First, note that by choice of
i^{\prime}
, Lemma
9.5
entails that we have

V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}=V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}}\quad\text{or}\quad V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}=V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}.

If
V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}=V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}}
, then

V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}}=V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}}\subseteq V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}.

Similarly, if
V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}=V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}
, then

V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}=V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}}\subseteq V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}}.

Hence, in particular we have

V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}}\subseteq V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}\quad\text{or}\quad V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}\subseteq V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}}.

Since Lemma
9.5
implies

J=\Big\lparen\bigcup_{1\leq i\leq\ell\colon i\neq i^{\prime}}V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{i}\Big\rparen\cap V=((V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}})\cup(V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}))\cap V,

this yields

(9.1)

J=V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V\quad\text{or}\quad J=V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}\cap V.

To see that
U\neq V
, we argue as follows.
Since
\mathcal{F}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{\ell}
is minimally
\mathfrak{c}
-sufficient, for

\mathcal{S}:=\mathcal{F}_{1}+\ldots+\mathcal{F}_{\ell}\quad\text{and}\quad\mathcal{S}_{i^{\prime}}:=\mathcal{F}_{1}+\ldots+\mathcal{F}_{i^{\prime}-1}+\mathcal{F}_{i^{\prime}+1}+\ldots+\mathcal{F}_{\ell},

we obtain
\mathcal{S}_{i^{\prime}}[U]\neq\mathcal{S}[V]
.
If there exists a vertex
v\in V\setminus U
, then
U\neq V
.
Thus, for our proof that
U\neq V
, we may assume that there exists an edge
e\in\mathcal{S}[V]\setminus\mathcal{S}_{i^{\prime}}[U]\subseteq\mathcal{F}_{i^{\prime}}[V\cap V_{i^{\prime}}]\setminus\mathcal{S}_{i^{\prime}}[U]
.
If
\lvert J\rvert\leq k-1
, then
\mathcal{F}_{i^{\prime}}[J]=\emptyset
and if
\lvert J\rvert\geq k
, then, since
F
is a subsequence of a vertex-separated loose path, due to (
9.1
), we have
\lvert J\rvert=k
and furthermore
\mathcal{F}_{i^{\prime}}[J]\subseteq\mathcal{F}_{i^{\prime}-1}[V\cap V_{i^{\prime}-1}]
or
\mathcal{F}_{i^{\prime}}[J]\subseteq\mathcal{F}_{i^{\prime}+1}[V\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}]
.
Hence, in any case, we have
\mathcal{F}_{i^{\prime}}[J]\subseteq\mathcal{S}_{i^{\prime}}[U]
and thus
e\in\mathcal{F}_{i^{\prime}}[V\cap V_{i^{\prime}}]\setminus\mathcal{F}_{i^{\prime}}[J]
.
This implies that there exists

v\in e\setminus J\subseteq(V\cap V_{i^{\prime}})\setminus J=(V\cap V_{i^{\prime}})\setminus U\subseteq V\setminus U,

so we have
U\neq V
.

It remains to prove that
\rho_{\mathcal{C},U}\leq\rho_{\mathcal{F}}
. To this end, let
\mathcal{A}:=\mathcal{F}_{i^{\prime}}[V\cap V_{i^{\prime}}]
and note that for all
1\leq i\leq\ell
with
i\neq i^{\prime}
and
f\in\mathcal{F}_{i}[V\cap V_{i}]
, we have
f\subseteq U
and hence
f\in\mathcal{C}[U]
.
Thus,

\mathcal{C}\setminus\mathcal{C}[U]=\Big\lparen\bigcup_{1\leq i\leq\ell}\mathcal{F}_{i}[V\cap V_{i}]\Big\rparen\setminus\mathcal{C}[U]=\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{C}[U]=\mathcal{A}\setminus(\mathcal{C}[U]\cap\mathcal{A})=\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[J].

Furthermore, for all
1\leq i\leq\ell
with
i\neq i^{\prime}
and
v\in V\cap V_{i}
, we have
v\in U
, so we also have

V\setminus U=\Big\lparen\bigcup_{1\leq i\leq\ell}V\cap V_{i}\Big\rparen\setminus U=(V\cap V_{i})\setminus U=(V\cap V_{i})\setminus J.

Thus,
\rho_{\mathcal{C},U}=\rho_{\mathcal{A},J}
.
Hence, since (
9.1
) states that we have
J=V_{\mathcal{A}}\cap V_{i^{\prime}-1}\cap V_{i^{\prime}}
or
J=V_{\mathcal{A}}\cap V_{i^{\prime}}\cap V_{i^{\prime}+1}
, Lemma
8.9
entails
\rho_{\mathcal{C},U}\leq\rho_{\mathcal{F}}
, which completes the proof as explained above.
∎

Lemma 9.7
.

Suppose that
F=\mathcal{F}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{\ell}
is minimally
\mathfrak{c}
-sufficient for some chain
\mathfrak{c}\in\mathfrak{C}
.
Then,
\ell\leq 1/\varepsilon^{4k}
.

Proof.

Suppose that
\mathfrak{c}=(V,F,I)
.
By Lemma
8.3
, we have
\lvert V\rvert\leq\varepsilon^{-3}
, hence
\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\rvert\leq\varepsilon^{-3k}
and thus, since
F
is minimally
\mathfrak{c}
-sufficient,
\ell\leq\varepsilon^{-3}+\varepsilon^{-3k}
.
∎

Lemma 9.8
.

Suppose that
\mathfrak{c}\in\mathfrak{C}
.
Let
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]
and
\beta\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e
where
f\in\mathcal{F}
.
Suppose that
\mathfrak{b}
is the
\beta
-branching of
\mathfrak{c}
.
Let
