e^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\beta}\setminus\mathopen{\{}e\mathclose{\}}
and
\beta^{\prime}\colon f^{\prime}\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e^{\prime}
where
f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}
.
Suppose that
\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}
is the
\beta^{\prime}
-branching of
\mathfrak{b}
.
Then,

\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}\leq\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}-1\quad\text{or}\quad\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}=\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}.

Furthermore, if
\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}=\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}
, then
\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
and
\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{b}}^{e^{\prime}}
are template equivalent.

Proof.

Suppose that
\mathfrak{b}=(F,V,I)
and
\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}=(F^{\prime},V^{\prime},I)
.
From all minimally
\mathfrak{b}
-sufficient sequences, choose
\mathcal{F}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{\ell-1}
such that
\chi_{\mathcal{F}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{\ell-1}}
is minimal.
Let
\mathcal{F}_{\ell}:=\mathcal{F}_{\mathfrak{b}}^{\beta^{\prime}}
.
For
1\leq i\leq\ell
, let
V_{i}:=V_{\mathcal{F}_{i}}
.
Observe that the sequence
\mathcal{F}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{\ell}
is
\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}
-sufficient.
Consider a minimally
\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}
-sufficient subsequence
\mathcal{F}_{i_{1}},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{i_{\ell^{\prime}}}
of
\mathcal{F}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{F}_{\ell}
with
i_{1}=1
.
Note that
i_{\ell^{\prime}}=\ell
.
To shorten notation, for
1\leq i,j\leq\ell
, we set

f(i,j):=\varepsilon^{5k\lvert V_{i}\cap V_{j}\rvert}.

For all
1\leq j\leq\ell^{\prime}-1
with
i_{j+1}=i_{j}+1
, we vacuously have

\sum_{i_{j}\leq i\leq i_{j+1}-1}f(i,i+1)=f(i_{j},i_{j+1})

and for all
1\leq j\leq\ell^{\prime}-2
with
i_{j+1}\geq i_{j}+2
, Lemma
9.6
together with Lemma
9.7
implies

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{j}\leq i\leq i_{j+1}-1}f(i,i+1)

\displaystyle\leq(i_{j+1}-i_{j})\varepsilon^{5k}f(i_{j},i_{j+1})\leq\varepsilon f(i_{j},i_{j+1})

\displaystyle=f(i_{j},i_{j+1})-(1-\varepsilon)f(i_{j},i_{j+1})\leq f(i_{j},i_{j+1})-\frac{\varepsilon^{5k^{2}}}{2}.

For

\Lambda:=\begin{cases}1&\text{if\penalty\ $i_{1},\ldots,i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}\neq 1,\ldots,\ell-2$};\\
0&\text{otherwise},\end{cases}

using that
V^{\prime}
is the disjoint union of
V^{\prime}\setminus V
and
V\cap V^{\prime}=V\setminus(V\setminus V^{\prime})
, this yields

\displaystyle\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq\lvert V^{\prime}\rvert-\varepsilon^{-5k(k+1)}\Big\lparen\sum_{1\leq j\leq\ell^{\prime}-2}f(i_{j},i_{j+1})\Big\rparen-\varepsilon^{-5k(k+1)}f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell)

\displaystyle\leq\lvert V^{\prime}\rvert-\varepsilon^{-5k(k+1)}\bigg\lparen\frac{\varepsilon^{5k^{2}}}{2}\Lambda+\sum_{1\leq j\leq\ell^{\prime}-2}\sum_{i_{j}\leq i\leq i_{j+1}-1}f(i,i+1)\bigg\rparen-\varepsilon^{-5k(k+1)}f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell)

\displaystyle=\lvert V^{\prime}\rvert-\varepsilon^{-5k(k+1)}\Big\lparen\sum_{1\leq i\leq i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}-1}f(i,i+1)\Big\rparen-\frac{\varepsilon^{-5k}}{2}\Lambda-\varepsilon^{-5k(k+1)}f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell)

\displaystyle=\lvert V\rvert+m-k-\lvert V\setminus V^{\prime}\rvert-\varepsilon^{-5k(k+1)}\Big\lparen\sum_{1\leq i\leq\ell-2}f(i,i+1)\Big\rparen

\displaystyle\hphantom{=}\mathrel{}\quad+\varepsilon^{-5k(k+1)}\Big\lparen\sum_{i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}\leq i\leq\ell-2}f(i,i+1)\Big\rparen-\frac{\varepsilon^{-5k}}{2}\Lambda-\varepsilon^{-5k(k+1)}f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell)

\displaystyle=\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}+m-k-\lvert V\setminus V^{\prime}\rvert+\varepsilon^{-5k(k+1)}\Big\lparen\sum_{i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}\leq i\leq\ell-2}f(i,i+1)\Big\rparen

\displaystyle\hphantom{=}\mathrel{}\quad-\varepsilon^{-5k(k+1)}f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell)-\frac{\varepsilon^{-5k}}{2}\Lambda.

Note that
i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}\leq\ell-3
,
i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}=\ell-2
or
i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}=\ell-1
.
We investigate the three cases separately.

First, suppose that
i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}\leq\ell-3
.
Then using Lemma
9.6
, Lemma
9.7
and Lemma
9.5
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}\leq i\leq\ell-2}f(i,i+1)

\displaystyle\leq(\ell-i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}-1)\varepsilon^{5k}f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell-1)\leq\varepsilon f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell-1)

\displaystyle=f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell-1)-(1-\varepsilon)f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell-1)\leq f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell-1)-\frac{\varepsilon^{5k^{2}}}{2}

\displaystyle\leq f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell)-\frac{\varepsilon^{5k^{2}}}{2}.

Hence, if
i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}\leq\ell-3
, then

\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}\leq\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}+m-k-\lvert V\setminus V^{\prime}\rvert-\frac{\varepsilon^{-5k}}{2}\leq\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}-1.

Next, suppose that
i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}=\ell-2
.
If

\lvert V_{i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}}\cap V_{\ell-1}\rvert\geq\lvert V_{i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}}\cap V_{\ell}\rvert+1,

then

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}\leq i\leq\ell-2}f(i,i+1)

\displaystyle=f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell-1)\leq\varepsilon^{5k}f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell)

\displaystyle=f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell)-(1-\varepsilon^{5k})f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell)\leq f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell)-\frac{\varepsilon^{5k^{2}}}{2}

and thus

\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}\leq\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}+m-k-\lvert V\setminus V^{\prime}\rvert-\frac{\varepsilon^{-5k}}{2}\leq\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}-1.

If

(9.2)

\lvert V_{i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}}\cap V_{\ell-1}\rvert\leq\lvert V_{i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}}\cap V_{\ell}\rvert,

then Lemma
9.5
entails

(9.3)

V_{i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}}\cap V_{\ell-1}=V_{i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}}\cap V_{\ell},

and thus

\sum_{i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}\leq i\leq\ell-2}f(i,i+1)=f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell-1)=f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell).

Due to Lemma
9.5
, a consequence of (
9.3
) is

V_{i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}}\cap V_{\ell-1}\subseteq V_{\ell-1}\cap V_{\ell}.

Since we assume that
i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}=\ell-2
, this yields

(9.4)

V_{\ell-1}\cap V^{\prime}\subseteq(V_{i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}}\cup V_{\ell})\cap V_{\ell-1}\subseteq V_{\ell}\cap V_{\ell-1}=e^{\prime}

and so in particular
\lvert V_{\ell-1}\cap V^{\prime}\rvert\leq k
and thus
\lvert V\setminus V^{\prime}\rvert\geq m-k
.
Hence, if (
9.2
) holds, then

(9.5)

\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}\leq\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}+m-k-\lvert V\setminus V^{\prime}\rvert-\frac{\varepsilon^{-5k}}{2}\Lambda\leq\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}-\frac{\varepsilon^{-5k}}{2}\Lambda

and thus
\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}\leq\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}-1
or
\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}=\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}
.

Finally, suppose that
i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}=\ell-1
.
Then,

\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}\leq\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}+m-k-\lvert V\setminus V^{\prime}\rvert-\varepsilon^{-5k(k+1)}f(i_{\ell^{\prime}-1},\ell)\leq\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}+m-k-\lvert V\setminus V^{\prime}\rvert-\varepsilon^{-5k}\leq\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}-1.

This finishes the analysis of the three cases and the proof that we have
\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}\leq\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}-1
or
\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}=\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}
.

It remains to further investigate the case where
\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}=\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}
.
Suppose that
\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}=\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}
.
Note that by Lemma
9.3
, it suffices to obtain that
\mathfrak{B}^{e}_{\mathfrak{b}|\mathord{-}}
and
\mathfrak{B}^{e^{\prime}}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}|\mathord{-}}
are template equivalent, so due to Lemma
9.4
, it suffices to show that
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}|\mathord{-}},I)
is a copy of
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}|\mathord{-}},I)
with
e^{\prime}
playing the role of
e
.
Our analysis of the three cases above shows that
\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}=\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}
is only possible if
i_{\ell^{\prime}-1}=\ell-2
, (
9.3
), (
9.4
) and (
9.5
) hold.
Revisiting the first inequality in (
9.5
), we see that
\Lambda=0
and
\lvert V\setminus V^{\prime}\rvert=m-k
necessarily hold.
Let
\mathcal{S}:=\mathcal{F}_{1}+\ldots+\mathcal{F}_{\ell-2}
, let
\mathcal{E}
denote the
k
-graph with vertex set
e
and edge set
\mathopen{\{}e\mathclose{\}}
and let
\mathcal{E}^{\prime}
denote the
k
-graph with vertex set
e^{\prime}
and edge set
\mathopen{\{}e^{\prime}\mathclose{\}}
.
Note that
\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}|\mathord{-}}=\mathcal{S}[V\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}]+\mathcal{E}
and that as a consequence of
\Lambda=0
, we have
\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}|\mathord{-}}=\mathcal{S}[V^{\prime}\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}]+\mathcal{E}^{\prime}
.
Thus, to see that
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}|\mathord{-}},I)
is a copy of
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}|\mathord{-}},I)
with
e^{\prime}
playing the role of
e
, it suffices to obtain
V\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}=V^{\prime}\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}
and additionally
e\cap V\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}=e^{\prime}\cap V^{\prime}\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}
.
Since (
9.4
) entails

V_{\ell-1}\setminus e^{\prime}\subseteq V_{\ell-1}\setminus(V_{\ell-1}\cap V^{\prime})=V_{\ell-1}\setminus V^{\prime}\subseteq V\setminus V^{\prime},

from
\lvert V\setminus V^{\prime}\rvert=m-k
, we obtain
V\setminus V^{\prime}=V_{\ell-1}\setminus e^{\prime}
and thus using (
9.3
), we have

\displaystyle(V\cap V_{\mathcal{S}})\setminus(V^{\prime}\cap V_{\mathcal{S}})

\displaystyle=(V\setminus V^{\prime})\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}=(V_{\ell-1}\setminus e^{\prime})\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}=(V_{\ell-1}\cap V_{\mathcal{S}})\setminus e^{\prime}=(V_{\ell-1}\cap V_{\ell-2})\setminus e^{\prime}

\displaystyle=(V_{\ell}\cap V_{\ell-1}\cap V_{\ell-2})\setminus e^{\prime}=\emptyset.

Since
V^{\prime}\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}\subseteq V\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}
, this yields

(9.6)

V\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}=V^{\prime}\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}.

Furthermore, again using (
9.3
), we obtain

e\cap V_{\ell-2}=V_{\ell-1}\cap V_{\ell-2}=V_{\ell}\cap V_{\ell-1}\cap V_{\ell-2}=e^{\prime}\cap V_{\ell-2}.

Combining this with (
9.6
) yields

e\cap V\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}=(e\cap V_{\ell-2})\cap V\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}=(e^{\prime}\cap V_{\ell-2})\cap V\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}=(e^{\prime}\cap V_{\ell-2})\cap V^{\prime}\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}=e^{\prime}\cap V_{\ell-1}\cap V_{\ell-2}\cap V^{\prime}\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}.

Since Lemma
9.5
entails
V_{\ell-1}\cap V_{i}\subseteq V_{\ell-1}\cap V_{\ell-2}
for all
1\leq i\leq\ell-2
, thus
V_{\ell-1}\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}\subseteq V_{\ell-1}\cap V_{\ell-2}
and hence
V_{\ell-1}\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}=V_{\ell-1}\cap V_{\ell-2}\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}
, this yields

e\cap V\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}=e^{\prime}\cap V_{\ell-1}\cap V^{\prime}\cap V_{\mathcal{S}}=e^{\prime}\cap V^{\prime}\cap V_{\mathcal{S}},

which completes the proof.
∎

9.2.
Tracking branching families

Suppose that
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
, consider a chain
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C}
and let
\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}
.
Let
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]
.
Similarly as in Section
8.3
, we show that
\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\Phi_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}},\psi}
is typically close to
\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}
, that is that

X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}^{e}:=\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\Phi_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}},\psi}-\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}=\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}

is typically small, where the quantification of the deviation we allow crucially relies on the insights from Section
9.1
.
Formally, we finally define the fifth stopping time mentioned in Section
6
as

\tau_{\mathfrak{B}}:=\min\mathopen{}\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}i\geq 0:&\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\Phi_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}},\psi}\neq\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\pm\varepsilon^{-\chi_{\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}\\
&\quad\text{for some }\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C},e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I],\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}\end{array}\right\}\mathclose{}

and we show that the probability that
\tau_{\mathfrak{B}}\leq\tau^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}
is small.
The following Lemma
9.9
shows that indeed
\tilde{\tau}_{\mathfrak{B}}\geq\tau_{\mathfrak{B}}
.
Similarly as in Section
8.3
, Lemma
9.11
shows that it suffices to consider a collection of branching families that has size at most
1/\delta
, which in turn allows us to restrict our attention to only one fixed branching family.
To prove Lemma
9.11
, we observe that there are only finitely many relevant error parameters (see Lemma
9.10
).

Lemma 9.9
.

Let
\mathfrak{c}\in\mathfrak{C}
.
Then,
\delta^{1/2}\leq\varepsilon^{-\chi_{\mathfrak{c}}}\leq\delta^{-1/2}
.

Proof.

Suppose that
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)
.
From Lemma
8.3
, we obtain
\chi_{\mathfrak{c}}\leq\lvert V\rvert\leq\varepsilon^{-3}
and from Lemma
9.7
, we obtain
\chi_{\mathfrak{c}}\geq-\varepsilon^{-5k(k+1)}\cdot\varepsilon^{-4k^{2}}
, so the statement follows.
∎

Lemma 9.10
.

The set
\mathopen{\{}\chi_{\mathfrak{c}}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\mathfrak{c}\in\mathfrak{C}\mathclose{\}}
is finite.

Proof.

As a consequence of Lemma
8.3
, it suffices to show that

X:=\mathopen{\{}\chi_{F}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\text{ $F$ is minimally\penalty\ $\mathfrak{c}$-sufficient for some\penalty\ $\mathfrak{c}\in\mathfrak{C}$ }\mathclose{\}}

is finite.
By Lemma
9.7
, every sequence that is minimally
\mathfrak{c}
-sufficient for some
\mathfrak{c}\in\mathfrak{C}
has length at most
\varepsilon^{-4k}
, which entails that
X
is indeed finite.
∎

Lemma 9.11
.

There exists a collection
\mathfrak{C}_{0}\subseteq\mathfrak{C}
with
\lvert\mathfrak{C}_{0}\rvert\leq 1/\delta
such that for all
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C}
and
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]
, there exist
\mathfrak{c}_{0}=(F_{0},V_{0},I_{0})\in\mathfrak{C}_{0}
and
e_{0}\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}_{0}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}_{0}}[I]
such that
\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}_{0}}^{e_{0}}
and
\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
are template equivalent with
\chi_{\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}_{0}}^{e_{0}}}\leq\chi_{\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}
.

Proof.

Similarly as in the proof of Lemma
8.17
, consider the set
\mathscr{T}
of all templates
(\mathcal{A},I)
where
V_{\mathcal{A}}\subseteq\mathopen{\{}1,\ldots,1/\varepsilon^{3}\mathclose{\}}
.
By Lemma
8.3
, for all
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C}
, we may choose a template
\mathcal{T}_{\mathfrak{c}}\in\mathscr{T}
that is a copy of
(\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},I)
.
For every chain
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)
and
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]
, we may consider the unordered
(\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!)
-tuple
\mathopen{\lparen}(\mathcal{T}_{\mathfrak{b}},\mathcal{T}_{\mathfrak{b}|\mathord{-}})\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}\mathclose{\rparen}
whose components are the pairs
(\mathcal{T}_{\mathfrak{b}},\mathcal{T}_{\mathfrak{b}|\mathord{-}})
of templates where
\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
.
We use
\mathscr{T}_{2}
to denote the set of such unordered tuples, that is we set

\mathscr{T}_{2}:=\mathopen{\{}\mathopen{\lparen}(\mathcal{T}_{\mathfrak{b}},\mathcal{T}_{\mathfrak{b}|\mathord{-}})\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}\mathclose{\rparen}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C},e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]\mathclose{\}}.

Note that
\lvert\mathscr{T}_{2}\rvert\leq\lvert\mathscr{T}\rvert^{2\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}
.
Consider an unordered tuple
\mathscr{P}\in\mathscr{T}_{2}
.
As a consequence of Lemma
9.10
, among all pairs
(\mathfrak{c},e)
where
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C}
and
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]
such that
\mathscr{P}=\mathopen{\lparen}(\mathcal{T}_{\mathfrak{b}},\mathcal{T}_{\mathfrak{b}|-})\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}\mathclose{\rparen}
, we may choose a pair
(\mathfrak{c}_{\mathscr{P}},e_{\mathscr{P}})
such that
\chi_{\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}_{\mathscr{P}}}^{e_{\mathscr{P}}}}
is minimal.
Then,
\mathopen{\{}\mathfrak{c}_{\mathscr{P}}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\mathscr{P}\in\mathscr{T}_{2}\mathclose{\}}
is a collection as desired.
∎

Observation 9.12
.

Suppose that
\mathfrak{C}_{0}\subseteq\mathfrak{C}
is a collection of chains as in Lemma
9.11
.
For
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C}
,
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]
and
\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}
, let

\tau_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}^{e}:=\min\mathopen{\Big\{}i\geq 0\mathrel{\Big\colonDelimiter}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\neq\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\pm\varepsilon^{-\chi_{\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}\mathclose{\Big\}}.

Then,

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau_{\mathfrak{B}}\leq\tau^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C}_{0},\\
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I],\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}^{e}\leq\tau^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}.

Hence, fix
\mathfrak{c}=(F,V,I)\in\mathfrak{C}
,
e\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}}[I]
and
\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}
and let
\chi:=\chi_{\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}
.
Besides
\mathfrak{c}
and
\psi
, we redefine several other symbols from Section
8
, for example
\xi_{0}
,
\xi_{1}
and
\tau
.
However, we still use some symbols from previous sections that we do not redefine.
Whenever we use a symbol, its most recent definition applies.
For
i\geq 0
, let

\xi_{1}(i):=\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\varepsilon^{-\chi}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I},\quad\xi_{0}(i):=(1-\delta)\xi_{1}

and define the stopping time

\tau:=\min\mathopen{\Big\{}i\geq 0\mathrel{\Big\colonDelimiter}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\Phi_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}},\psi}\neq\Big\lparen\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\Big\rparen\pm\xi_{1}\mathclose{\Big\}}.

Define the critical intervals

I^{-}(i):=\Big[\Big\lparen\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\Big\rparen-\xi_{1},\Big\lparen\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\Big\rparen-\xi_{0}\Big],\quad I^{+}(i):=\Big[\Big\lparen\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\Big\rparen+\xi_{0},\Big\lparen\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\Big\rparen+\xi_{1}\Big].

For
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
, let

Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i):={\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}^{e}-\xi_{1}.

For
i_{0}\geq 0
, define the stopping time

\tau^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}:=\min\mathopen{\Big\{}i\geq i_{0}\mathrel{\Big\colonDelimiter}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\Phi_{\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{c}},\psi}\notin I^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{\Big\}}

and for
i\geq i_{0}
, let

Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i):=Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i_{0}\vee(i\wedge\tau_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\wedge\tau^{\star}\wedge i^{\star})).

Let

\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}:=\min\mathopen{\{}j\geq 0\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}^{e}\geq\xi_{0}\text{ for all }j\leq i<\tau^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{\}}\leq\tau^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}.

With this setup, similarly as in Section
8.3
, it in fact suffices to consider the evolution of
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}),Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}+1),\ldots
.

Observation 9.13
.

\mathopen{\{}\tau\leq\tau^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{\}}\subseteq\mathopen{\{}Z^{-}_{\sigma^{-}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{\}}\cup\mathopen{\{}Z^{+}_{\sigma^{+}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{\}}
.

We again use Lemma
8.20
to show that the probabilities of the events on the right in Observation
9.13
are sufficiently small.

9.2.1.
Trend

Here, we prove that for all
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
and
i_{0}\geq 0
, the expected one-step changes of the process
Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i_{0}),Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i_{0}+1),\ldots
are non-positive.
Branching families are closely related to individual chains, so we may use statements from Section
8.3
as a starting point for our arguments here.
As a consequence of Lemma
8.22
, we obtain Lemma
9.14
where we state estimates for the one-step changes of the error term that we use in this section.
Using these estimates, we turn to proving that the process we consider here is indeed a supermartingale (see Lemma
9.18
).
We prove this by revisiting the expression for individual chains stated in Lemma
8.29
where, since we are now in the setting of branching families, we may now exploit that one step-changes depend on branching families.
This allows us to no longer differentiate between the different branchings as they always appear in complete families.
This ultimately enables us to identify self-correcting behavior as desired as a consequence of our careful choice of error parameters crucially relying on the insights from Section
9.1
.

Note that for
\mathfrak{b},\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
, we have
\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}}\rvert=\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}\rvert
.
Hence, we may choose
b
such that
b=\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}}\rvert
for all
\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
.

Lemma 9.14
.

Let
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i\leq\tau_{\emptyset}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\Delta\xi_{1}=_{\mathcal{X}}-\bigg\lparen b-1-\frac{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\xi_{1}}{H}\pm\frac{\zeta^{2}\xi_{1}}{H}.

Proof.

For
\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}
, we may apply Lemma
8.22
with
\mathfrak{b}
playing the role of
\mathfrak{c}
to obtain

\Delta(\delta^{-1}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I})=-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}}\rvert-1-\frac{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\delta^{-1}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}{H}\pm\frac{\zeta^{2}\delta^{-1}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}{H}.

This yields

\Delta\xi_{1}=\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}}\varepsilon^{-\chi}\delta\Delta(\delta^{-1}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I})=_{\mathcal{X}}-\bigg\lparen b-1-\frac{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}}\varepsilon^{-\chi}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}{H}\pm\frac{\zeta^{2}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}}\varepsilon^{-\chi}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}{H},

which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 9.15
.

Let
0\leq i_{0}\leq i
and
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}\leq 0
.

Proof.

Suppose that
i<i^{\star}
and let
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tau_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\wedge\tau^{\star}\mathclose{\}}
.
We have
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}^{\mathsf{c}}}0
and
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}
, so it suffices to obtain
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}0
.
From Lemma
8.24
, using Lemma
9.9
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta({\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}^{e})\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle={\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\bigg\lparen-\frac{(\lvert\mathcal{C}_{\mathfrak{b}}\rvert-1)\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}-\bigg\lparen\sum_{e^{\prime}\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{b}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{b}}\mathclose{\}}}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{b}}^{e^{\prime}}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}{k!\,H\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime},I}}X_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime},\psi}\bigg\rparen+\delta^{2}\frac{\delta^{-1}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}{H}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\bigg\lparen(b-1)({\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}^{e})+\frac{1}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}\bigg\lparen\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\sum_{e^{\prime}\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{b}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{b}}\mathclose{\}}}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{b}}^{e^{\prime}}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime},I}}({\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime},\psi})\bigg\rparen-\varepsilon\xi_{1}\bigg\rparen

Note that for all
\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
,
e^{\prime}\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{b}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{b}}\mathclose{\}}
and
\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}_{1},\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}_{2}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{b}}^{e^{\prime}}
, we have
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}_{1},I}=\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}_{2},I}
, so we may choose
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}^{e^{\prime}}
such that
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}^{e^{\prime}}=\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime},I}
for all
\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{b}}^{e^{\prime}}
.
With Lemma
8.3
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta({\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}^{e})\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\bigg\lparen(b-1)({\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}^{e})+\frac{1}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}\bigg\lparen\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\sum_{e^{\prime}\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{b}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{b}}\mathclose{\}}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}^{e^{\prime}}}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{b}}^{e^{\prime}}}{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime},\psi}\bigg\rparen-\varepsilon\xi_{1}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\bigg\lparen(b-1)(1-\delta)\xi_{1}+\frac{1}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}\bigg\lparen\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\sum_{e^{\prime}\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{b}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{b}}\mathclose{\}}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}^{e^{\prime}}}({\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}^{e^{\prime}})\bigg\rparen-\varepsilon\xi_{1}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\bigg\lparen(b-1)\xi_{1}+\frac{1}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}\bigg\lparen\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\sum_{e^{\prime}\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{b}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{b}}\mathclose{\}}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}^{e^{\prime}}}({\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}^{e^{\prime}})\bigg\rparen-\varepsilon^{1/2}\xi_{1}\bigg\rparen.

Thus, due to Lemma
9.14
, we have

(9.7)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\bigg\lparen\frac{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\xi_{1}+\frac{1}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}\bigg\lparen\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\sum_{e^{\prime}\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{b}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{b}}\mathclose{\}}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}^{e^{\prime}}}({\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}^{e^{\prime}})\bigg\rparen-\varepsilon^{1/3}\xi_{1}\bigg\rparen.

Note that for all
\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
and
e^{\prime}\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{b}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{b}}\mathclose{\}}
, if
\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}
and
\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{b}}^{e^{\prime}}
are template equivalent, then

{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}^{e^{\prime}}={\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}^{e}\geq_{\mathcal{X}}\xi_{0}\geq 0

and otherwise, Lemma
9.8
implies

\lvert{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}^{e^{\prime}}\rvert\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{b}}^{e^{\prime}}}\varepsilon^{-\chi_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}}}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime},I}\leq\varepsilon\sum_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{b}}^{e^{\prime}}}\varepsilon^{-\chi_{\mathfrak{b}}}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\prime},I}=\varepsilon\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\cdot\varepsilon^{-\chi}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}^{e^{\prime}}.

Hence, in any case,

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\sum_{e^{\prime}\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{b}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{b}}\mathclose{\}}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}^{e^{\prime}}}({\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}^{e^{\prime}})

\displaystyle\geq_{\mathcal{X}}-\varepsilon\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\sum_{e^{\prime}\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{b}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{b}}\mathclose{\}}}\varepsilon^{-\chi}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}=-\varepsilon\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\lvert\mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{b}}\setminus\mathopen{\{}J_{\mathfrak{b}}\mathclose{\}}\rvert\xi_{1}

\displaystyle\geq-\varepsilon^{1/2}\xi_{1}.

Consequently, returning to (
9.7
), we obtain

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\bigg\lparen\frac{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\xi_{1}-\varepsilon^{1/2}\xi_{1}-\varepsilon^{1/3}\xi_{1}\bigg\rparen\leq 0,

which completes the proof.
∎

9.2.2.
Boundedness

Here, we transfer the relevant results from Section
8.3.2
for individual chains, namely Lemma
8.27
, Lemma
8.28
and Lemma
8.30
, to branching families.

Lemma 9.16
.

Let
0\leq i_{0}\leq i\leq i^{\star}
,
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}^{\prime}}\wedge\tau_{\mathfrak{C}}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\lvert\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\leq n^{\varepsilon^{3}}\frac{\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}(i_{0})}{n\hat{p}(i_{0})^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}.

Proof.

Combining Lemma
9.14
and Lemma
8.26
, we obtain

\displaystyle\lvert\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\Big\lparen\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\lvert\Delta X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\rvert\Big\rparen+\lvert\Delta\xi_{1}\rvert\leq_{\mathcal{X}}n^{\varepsilon^{4}}\frac{\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}(i_{0})}{n\hat{p}(i_{0})^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}+\frac{\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}{H}

\displaystyle\leq n^{\varepsilon^{4}}\frac{\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}(i_{0})}{n\hat{p}(i_{0})^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}+\frac{\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}(i_{0})}{H(i_{0})}

With Lemma
7.9
, this completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 9.17
.

Let
0\leq i_{0}\leq i\leq i^{\star}
and
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\lvert\Delta Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\leq n^{\varepsilon^{3}}\frac{\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}(i_{0})}{n\hat{p}(i_{0})^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}}.

Proof.

This is an immediate consequence of Lemma
9.16
.
∎

Lemma 9.18
.

Let
0\leq i_{0}\leq i^{\star}
and
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,
\sum_{i\geq i_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq n^{\varepsilon^{3}}\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}
.

Proof.

Suppose that
i_{0}\leq i<i^{\star}
and let
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tau_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}^{\prime}}\wedge\tau_{\mathfrak{C}}\mathclose{\}}
.
We have
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}^{\mathsf{c}}}0
and with Lemma
9.14
, Lemma
8.29
and Lemma
7.7
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq\Big\lparen\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta X_{\mathfrak{b},\psi}\rvert\mathclose{]}\Big\rparen+\lvert\Delta\xi_{1}\rvert\leq_{\mathcal{X}}n^{\varepsilon^{4}}\frac{\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}+\frac{\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}{H}

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}n^{\varepsilon^{3}}\frac{\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}\leq n^{\varepsilon^{3}}\frac{\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}(i_{0})}{n^{k}\hat{p}(i_{0})}.

Thus,

\sum_{i\geq i_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\mathclose{]}=\sum_{i_{0}\leq i\leq i^{\star}-1}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq(i^{\star}-i_{0})n^{\varepsilon^{3}}\frac{\sum_{\mathfrak{b}\in\mathfrak{B}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{e}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathfrak{b},I}(i_{0})}{n^{k}\hat{p}(i_{0})}.

Since

i^{\star}-i_{0}\leq\frac{\vartheta n^{k}}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}-i_{0}=\frac{n^{k}\hat{p}(i_{0})}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}\leq n^{k}\hat{p}(i_{0}),

this completes the proof.
∎

9.2.3.
Supermartingale argument

This section follows a similar structure as Section
8.3.3
.
Lemma
9.19
is the final ingredient that we use for our application of Lemma
8.20
in the proof of Lemma
9.20
where we show that the probabilities of the events on the right in Observation
9.13
are indeed small.

Lemma 9.19
.

Let
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})\leq-\delta^{2}\xi_{1}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})
.

Proof.

Together with Lemma
8.1
, Lemma
7.4
implies
\tau^{\star}\geq 1
and
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}^{e}(0)<\xi_{0}(0)
, so we have
\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\geq 1
.
Thus, by definition of
\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}
, for
i:=\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}-1
, we have
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}^{e}\leq\xi_{0}
and thus

Z_{i}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}={\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}X_{\mathfrak{c},\psi}^{e}-\xi_{1}\leq-\delta\xi_{1}.

Furthermore, since
\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\leq\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}^{\prime}}\wedge\tau_{\mathfrak{C}}
, we may apply Lemma
9.16
to obtain

Z_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})=Z_{i}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}+\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\leq Z_{i}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}+\delta^{2}\xi_{1}\leq-\delta\xi_{1}+\delta^{2}\xi_{1}\leq-\delta^{2}\xi_{1}.

Since Lemma
8.5
entails
\Delta\xi_{1}\leq 0
, this completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 9.20
.

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau_{\mathfrak{B}}\leq\tau^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-n^{\varepsilon^{3}})
.

Proof.

Considering Observation
9.12
, it suffices to show that

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau\leq\tau^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-n^{2\varepsilon^{3}}).

Hence, by Observation
9.13
, is suffices to show that for
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
, we have
