H(\tau_{\emptyset})\geq_{\mathcal{E}_{0}^{\mathsf{c}}}\varepsilon H(i^{\star})\geq\varepsilon^{2}n^{k}\hat{p}(i^{\star})\geq n^{k-1/\rho_{\mathcal{F}}-2\varepsilon^{2}}

and hence
\mathcal{E}_{0}^{\mathsf{c}}\cap\mathcal{B}=\emptyset
.
Thus, using Lemma
13.16
, we obtain

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\mathcal{B}\mathclose{]}\leq\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z\leq n^{k-1/\rho_{\mathcal{F}}-\varepsilon}\mathclose{]}+\exp(-n^{1/3}).

With Lemma
14.3
and Chernoff’s inequality (see Lemma
14.4
), this completes the proof.
∎

15.
Proofs for the main theorems

In this section, we show how to obtain Theorems
1.2
–
1.5
from Theorems
5.1
and
13.1
.
Proofs for Theorems
1.6
and
1.7
can be found in Appendix
D
.

Proof of Theorem
1.4
.

This is an immediate consequence of Theorem
5.1
.
∎

Proof of Theorem
1.5
.

By definition of
\tau_{\emptyset}
in Section
13
, this is an immediate consequence of Theorem
13.1
.
∎

Proof of Theorem
1.3
.

Let
m:=\lvert V_{\mathcal{F}}\rvert
.
Suppose that
0<\varepsilon<1
is sufficiently small in terms of
1/m
, that
0<\delta<1
is sufficiently small in terms of
\varepsilon
and that
n
is sufficiently large in terms of
1/\delta
.
Suppose that
\mathcal{H}
is an
(\varepsilon^{20},\delta,\rho)
-pseudorandom
k
-graph on
n
vertices with
\lvert\mathcal{H}\rvert\geq n^{k-1/\rho+\varepsilon^{5}}
.
Let

\vartheta:=\frac{k!\,\lvert\mathcal{H}\rvert}{n^{k}}\geq n^{-1/\rho+\varepsilon^{5}}.

We consider the
\mathcal{F}
-removal process starting at
\mathcal{H}
where we assume the generated hypergraphs to remain constant if the process normally terminated due to the absence of copies of
\mathcal{F}
.
Let
\mathcal{H}^{\prime}
denote the
k
-graph generated after
i^{\star}
iterations, where

i^{\star}:=\frac{(\vartheta-n^{-1/\rho+\varepsilon^{5}})n^{k}}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}.

Let
\mathcal{H}^{\prime\prime}
denote the
k
-graph eventually generated by the process that contains no copies of
\mathcal{F}
as subgraphs.
Let
\mathcal{X}^{\prime}
denote the event that
\mathcal{H}^{\prime}
is
(4m,n^{\varepsilon^{4}})
-bounded,
\mathcal{F}
-populated and has
n^{k-1/\rho+\varepsilon^{5}}/k!
edges.
Let

\mathcal{X}^{\prime\prime}:=\mathopen{\{}\lvert\mathcal{H}^{\prime\prime}\rvert\leq n^{k-1/\rho+\varepsilon}\mathclose{\}}\quad\text{and}\quad\mathcal{Y}^{\prime\prime}:=\mathopen{\{}n^{k-1/\rho-\varepsilon}\leq\lvert\mathcal{H}^{\prime\prime}\rvert\mathclose{\}}.

We need to show that

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\mathcal{X}^{\prime\prime}\cap\mathcal{Y}^{\prime\prime}\mathclose{]}\geq 1-\exp(-(\log n)^{5/4}).

Since
\mathcal{X}^{\prime}\subseteq\mathcal{X}^{\prime\prime}
, we have
\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\mathcal{X}^{\prime\prime}\cap\mathcal{Y}^{\prime\prime}\mathclose{]}\geq\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\mathcal{X}^{\prime}\cap\mathcal{Y}^{\prime\prime}\mathclose{]}
, so it suffices to obtain sufficiently large lower bounds for
\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\mathcal{X}^{\prime}\mathclose{]}
and
\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\mathcal{Y}^{\prime\prime}\mathclose{]}
.
We may apply Theorem
5.1
with
\varepsilon^{5}
playing the role of
\varepsilon
to obtain
\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\mathcal{X}^{\prime}\mathclose{]}\geq 1-\exp(-(\log n)^{4/3})
and Theorem
13.1
shows that
\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\mathcal{Y}^{\prime\prime}\mathrel{|}\mathcal{X}^{\prime}\mathclose{]}\geq 1-\exp(-n^{1/4})
.
Using
\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\mathcal{Y}^{\prime\prime}\mathclose{]}=\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\mathcal{Y}^{\prime\prime}\mathrel{|}\mathcal{X}^{\prime}\mathclose{]}\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\mathcal{X}^{\prime}\mathclose{]}
, this yields suitable lower bounds for
\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\mathcal{X}^{\prime}\mathclose{]}
and
\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\mathcal{Y}^{\prime\prime}\mathclose{]}
.
∎

Proof of Theorem
1.2
.

This is an immediate consequence of Theorem
1.3
.
∎

16.
Concluding remarks

For both, the
\mathcal{F}
-free process and the
\mathcal{F}
-removal process, the number of edges present at step
i
of the process, that is, after
i
iterations, is a deterministic quantity.
Heuristically, intuition suggests that the set of edges present at step
i
behaves as if it was obtained by including every
k
-set of vertices independently at random with an appropriate probability
p
.

For the
\mathcal{F}
-free process on
n
vertices, we have
p\approx k!\,i/n^{k}
.
There are approximately
(1-p)n^{k}/k!
potential edges that are not yet present.
Using
v(\mathcal{F})
to denote the number of vertices,
e(\mathcal{F})
to denote the number of edges, and
\aut(\mathcal{F})
to denote the number of automorphisms of
\mathcal{F}
, for every such edge
e
, the expected number of copies of
\mathcal{F}
that that would be generated by adding
e
is
e(\mathcal{F})k!\,n^{v(\mathcal{F})-k}p^{e(\mathcal{F})-1}/\aut(\mathcal{F})
.
Hence, the Poisson paradigm suggests that the number of potential edges that are available for addition in a later step is approximately

(1-p)\exp\bigg\lparen-\frac{e(\mathcal{F})k!\,n^{v(\mathcal{F})-k}p^{e(\mathcal{F})-1}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})}\bigg\rparen\frac{n^{k}}{k!}.

This number becomes negligible compared to the approximate number
n^{k}p/k!
of present edges when

p=\Bigg\lparen\bigg\lparen\frac{\aut(\mathcal{F})(v(\mathcal{F})-k)}{e(\mathcal{F})(e(\mathcal{F})-1)k!}\bigg\rparen^{\frac{1}{e(\mathcal{F})-1}}\pm o(1)\Bigg\rparen(\log n)^{\frac{1}{e(\mathcal{F})-1}}n^{-\frac{v(\mathcal{F})-k}{e(\mathcal{F})-1}}.

Hence, we conjecture the following.

Conjecture 16.1
.

Let
k\geq 2
and consider a strictly
k
-balanced
k
-uniform hypergraph
\mathcal{F}
with
k
-density
\rho
.
Then, for all
\varepsilon>0
, there exists
n_{0}\geq 0
such that for all
n\geq n_{0}
, with probability at least
1-\varepsilon
, we have

F(n,\mathcal{F})=\Bigg\lparen\frac{1}{k!}\bigg\lparen\frac{\aut(\mathcal{F})(v(\mathcal{F})-k)}{e(\mathcal{F})(e(\mathcal{F})-1)k!}\bigg\rparen^{\frac{1}{e(\mathcal{F})-1}}\pm\varepsilon\Bigg\rparen(\log n)^{\frac{1}{e(\mathcal{F})-1}}n^{k-\frac{v(\mathcal{F})-k}{e(\mathcal{F})-1}}.

The known bounds for the case where
\mathcal{F}
is a triangle, see
[
8
,
12
]
, match this prediction and it would be interesting to further investigate other cases.
Conjecture
16.1
is closely related to
[
7
, Conjecture 13.1]
.

Again following the above heuristic, for the
\mathcal{F}
-removal process we have
p\approx 1-e(\mathcal{F})k!\,i/n^{k}
such that again, there are approximately
n^{k}p/k!
edges present.
Let
\mathcal{H}^{*}
denote the auxiliary hypergraph where the present edges are the vertices and where the edges sets of present copies are the edges.
Let
H^{*}
denote the number of edges of
\mathcal{H}^{*}
, that is the number of remaining copies of
\mathcal{F}
.
We expect the
2
-degrees in
\mathcal{H}^{*}
, that is the number of edges in
\mathcal{H}^{*}
that contain two fixed vertices of
\mathcal{H}
, to be generally negligible compared to the vertex degrees in
\mathcal{H}^{*}
.
Hence for the probability that a fixed present copy
\mathcal{F}^{\prime}
of
\mathcal{F}
is no longer present in the next step, we estimate

\frac{(\sum_{e\in E(\mathcal{F}^{\prime})}d_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}(e))-e(\mathcal{F})+1}{H^{*}}.

Then, using
\mathscr{F}_{0},\mathscr{F}_{1},\ldots
to denote the natural filtration associated with the process, for the expected one-step change
\mathbb{E}\mathopen{[}\Delta H^{*}\mathrel{|}\mathscr{F}_{i}\mathclose{]}
of
H^{*}
, we obtain

\mathbb{E}\mathopen{[}\Delta H^{*}\mathrel{|}\mathscr{F}_{i}\mathclose{]}\approx-\sum_{\mathcal{F}^{\prime}\in E(\mathcal{H}^{*})}\frac{(\sum_{e\in E(\mathcal{F}^{\prime})}d_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}(e))-e(\mathcal{F})+1}{H^{*}}=-\frac{1}{H^{*}}\Big\lparen\sum_{d\geq 0}d_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}(e)^{2}\Big\rparen+e(\mathcal{F})-1.

We expect the degrees in
\mathcal{H}^{*}
to be Poisson distributed and mutually independent.
Thus, since the average degree in
\mathcal{H}^{*}
is approximately
\lambda:=e(\mathcal{F})k!\,H^{*}/(n^{k}p)
, we expect that for all
d\geq 0
, the random variable
\lvert\mathopen{\{}e\in\mathcal{H}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}d_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}(e)=d\mathclose{\}}\rvert
is concentrated around

\frac{n^{k}p}{k!}\cdot\frac{\lambda^{d}\exp(-\lambda)}{d!}.

Thus, we estimate

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathopen{[}\Delta H^{*}\mathrel{|}\mathscr{F}_{i}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle\approx-\frac{1}{H^{*}}\Big\lparen\sum_{d\geq 0}d^{2}\lvert\mathopen{\{}e\in\mathcal{H}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}d_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}(e)=d\mathclose{\}}\rvert\Big\rparen+e(\mathcal{F})-1

\displaystyle\approx-\frac{n^{k}p}{k!\,H^{*}}\Big\lparen\sum_{d\geq 0}d^{2}\cdot\frac{\lambda^{d}\exp(-\lambda)}{d!}\Big\rparen+e(\mathcal{F})-1=-\frac{n^{k}p}{k!\,H^{*}}(\lambda^{2}+\lambda)+e(\mathcal{F})-1

\displaystyle=-\frac{e(\mathcal{F})^{2}k!H^{*}}{n^{k}p}-1.

We expect the number of present copies to typically closely follow a deterministic trajectory
\hat{h}^{*}_{0},\hat{h}^{*}_{1},\ldots
which by our above argument should satisfy

\hat{h}^{*}_{i+1}-\hat{h}^{*}_{i}\approx-\frac{e(\mathcal{F})^{2}k!\hat{h}^{*}_{i}}{n^{k}p}-1.

Guided by this intuition, for
i\geq 0
, we obtain an expression for
\hat{h}^{*}_{i}
by solving the corresponding differential equation.
Specifically, since initially there are approximately
n^{v(\mathcal{F})}/\aut(\mathcal{F})
copies of
\mathcal{F}
in
K_{n}^{(k)}
, we set

\hat{h}^{*}_{i}:=\frac{n^{v(\mathcal{F})}p^{e(\mathcal{F})}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})}-\frac{n^{k}p}{e(\mathcal{F})(e(\mathcal{F})-1)k!}.

This quantity becomes zero when

p=\Bigg\lparen\bigg\lparen\frac{\aut(\mathcal{F})}{e(\mathcal{F})(e(\mathcal{F})-1)k!}\bigg\rparen^{\frac{1}{e(\mathcal{F})-1}}\pm o(1)\Bigg\rparen n^{-\frac{v(\mathcal{F})-k}{e(\mathcal{F})-1}}.

Hence, for the
\mathcal{F}
-removal process, we conjecture the following.

Conjecture 16.2
.

Let
k\geq 2
and consider a strictly
k
-balanced
k
-uniform hypergraph
\mathcal{F}
with
k
-density
\rho
.
Then, for all
\varepsilon>0
, there exists
n_{0}\geq 0
such that for all
n\geq n_{0}
, with probability at least
1-\varepsilon
, we have

R(n,\mathcal{F})=\Bigg\lparen\frac{1}{k!}\bigg\lparen\frac{\aut(\mathcal{F})}{e(\mathcal{F})(e(\mathcal{F})-1)k!}\bigg\rparen^{\frac{1}{e(\mathcal{F})-1}}\pm\varepsilon\Bigg\rparen n^{k-\frac{v(\mathcal{F})-k}{e(\mathcal{F})-1}}.

Theorem
1.2
confirms the order of magnitude in this conjecture whenever
\mathcal{H}
is strictly
k
-balanced.
It would be interesting to obtain more precise results and to confirm the asymptotic value of the constant factor.

The
\mathcal{F}
-free process where
\mathcal{F}
is a diamond, which is a graph that is not strictly
2
-balanced, typically terminates with a final number of edges that has a different exponent for the logarithmic factor compared to Conjecture
16.1
, see
[
22
]
.
Hence, for the
\mathcal{F}
-free process as well as the
\mathcal{F}
-removal process, it could be interesting to further investigate the situation for graphs or hypergraphs that are not (strictly) balanced.

In terms of applications, the conjectures above suggest that the
\mathcal{F}
-free process is more suitable for generating dense
\mathcal{F}
-free graphs, however, the
\mathcal{F}
-removal process might prove to be a useful tool for decomposition and packing problems since it carefully constructs a maximal collection of edge-disjoint copies of
\mathcal{F}
.
For such applications, we believe that the fact that we do not require the initial hypergraph to be complete might be crucial.

Additionally, as we believe that such an extension could be useful for applications, we remark that directly using Lemma
10.1
instead of one of the theorems makes it possible to easily amend our analysis as follows if the goal is to show that the random graphs generated by the process typically exhibit further properties that we did not consider in our analysis.

Similarly to how we organized our analysis by using stopping times, one may define a stopping time
\tau
that measures when the desired property is first violated.
Then for
\tau^{\star}
and
i^{\star}
as defined in Lemma
10.1
, it suffices show that
\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau\leq\tau^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}
is small as this entails that
\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau\wedge\tau^{\star}\leq i^{\star}\mathclose{]}
is small and hence that the process typically runs for at least
i^{\star}
steps while maintaining the desired property.
For example, it is easy to see that in fact, typically a more precise estimate for the number of copies of
\mathcal{F}
in every step holds provided that the guarantees concerning the initial hypergraph are more precise.
This might be useful for counting the number of choices available for every deletion which can in turn be useful for counting the number of packings of edge-disjoint copies of
\mathcal{F}
.
Specifically, instead of only obtaining
\hat{h}^{*}(i)\pm\zeta(i)^{1+\varepsilon^{3}}
as an estimate for the number of copies present after
i
deletions as in our first part of the proof, it is possible to instead obtain
\hat{h}^{*}(i)\pm\delta^{-6}\zeta(i)^{2}
if a slightly more precise estimate holds for
i=0
.
To obtain this refinement following an approach as mentioned above, it suffices use the same argumentation that proves Lemma
7.20

(i)
with only minor adaptations.

Appendix A
Counting copies of
\mathcal{F}

.

.
Our approach is similar as in Sections

and

.

Observation A.1
.

\mathopen{\{}\tau_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{\}}\subseteq\mathopen{\{}Z^{-}_{\sigma^{-}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{\}}\cup\mathopen{\{}Z^{+}_{\sigma^{+}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{\}}
.

We again use supermartingale concentration techniques to show that the probabilities of the events on the right in Observation
A.1
are sufficiently small.
However, instead of relying on Freedman’s inequality, here, similarly as in Section
13
, we instead use Azuma’s inequality.

A.1.
Trend

Here, we prove that for all
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
and
i_{0}\geq 0
, the expected one-step changes of the process
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}(i_{0}),Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}(i_{0}+1),\ldots
are non-positive.
We begin with estimating the one-step changes of the deterministic parts of this random process in Lemma
A.3
.
Using Lemma
7.21
, we obtain Lemma
A.4
where we provide a precise estimate for the expected one-step change of the non-deterministic part that holds whenever the removal process was well-behaved up to the step we consider.
Finally, we combine our estimates for the deterministic and non-deterministic parts to see that the above process is indeed a supermartingale (see Lemma
A.5
).

Observation A.2
.

Extend
\hat{p}
,
\hat{h}^{*}
and
\eta_{1}
to continuous trajectories defined on the whole interval
[0,i^{\star}+1]
using the same expressions as above.
Then, for
x\in[0,i^{\star}+1]
,

\begin{gathered}(\hat{h}^{*})^{\prime}(x)=-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}k!\,\hat{h}^{*}(x)}{n^{k}\hat{p}(x)},\quad(\hat{h}^{*})^{\prime\prime}(x)=\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{3}(\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert-1)(k!)^{2}\hat{h}^{*}(x)}{n^{2k}\hat{p}(x)^{2}},\\
\eta_{1}^{\prime}(x)=-\frac{\big\lparen\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert-\frac{(1+\varepsilon^{3})\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\big\rparen\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\eta_{1}(x)}{n^{k}\hat{p}(x)},\\
\eta_{1}^{\prime\prime}(x)=-\frac{\big\lparen\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert-\frac{(1+\varepsilon^{3})\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\big\rparen\big\lparen\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert-\frac{(1+\varepsilon^{3})\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}-1\big\rparen\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}(k!)^{2}\eta_{1}(x)}{n^{2k}\hat{p}(x)^{2}}.\end{gathered}

Lemma A.3
.

Let
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i\leq\tau_{\emptyset}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\Delta\hat{h}^{*}=_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}\hat{h}^{*}}{H}\pm\frac{\zeta^{2+\varepsilon^{2}}\hat{h}^{*}}{H},\quad\Delta\eta_{1}=_{\mathcal{X}}-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert-\frac{(1+\varepsilon^{3})\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\eta_{1}}{H}\pm\frac{\zeta^{2+\varepsilon^{2}}\eta_{1}}{H}.

Proof.

This is a consequence of Taylor’s theorem.
In detail, we argue as follows.

Together with Observation
A.2
, Lemma
7.10
yields

\Delta\hat{h}^{*}=-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}k!\,\hat{h}^{*}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}\pm\max_{x\in[i,i+1]}\frac{\hat{h}^{*}(x)}{\delta n^{2k}\hat{p}(x)^{2}}.

We investigate the first term and the maximum separately.
Using Lemma
7.7
, we have

-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}k!\,\hat{h}^{*}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}=_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}\hat{h}^{*}}{H}.

Furthermore, since
\hat{h}^{*}(x)/\hat{p}(x)^{2}
is non-decreasing in
x
for
x\in[i,i+1]
, Lemma
7.7
together with Lemma
7.9
yields

\max_{x\in[i,i+1]}\frac{\hat{h}^{*}(x)}{\delta n^{2k}\hat{p}(x)^{2}}\leq\frac{\hat{h}^{*}}{\delta n^{2k}\hat{p}^{2}}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{\hat{h}^{*}}{\delta H^{2}}\leq\frac{\zeta^{2+2\varepsilon^{2}}\hat{h}^{*}}{\delta H}\leq\frac{\zeta^{2+\varepsilon^{2}}\hat{h}^{*}}{H}.

Thus we obtain the desired expression for
\Delta\hat{h}^{*}
.

We argue similarly for
\Delta\eta_{1}
.
Again together with Observation
A.2
, Lemma
7.10
yields

\Delta\eta_{1}=-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert-\frac{(1+\varepsilon^{3})\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\eta_{1}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}\pm\max_{x\in[i,i+1]}\frac{\eta_{1}(x)}{\delta n^{2k}\hat{p}(x)^{2}}.

We again investigate the first term and the maximum separately.
Using Lemma
7.7
, we have

-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert-\frac{(1+\varepsilon^{3})\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\eta_{1}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}=_{\mathcal{X}}-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert-\frac{(1+\varepsilon^{3})\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\eta_{1}}{H}.

Furthermore, using Lemma
7.6
, Lemma
7.7
and Lemma
7.9
yields

\max_{x\in[i,i+1]}\frac{\eta_{1}(x)}{\delta n^{2k}\hat{p}(x)^{2}}\leq\frac{\eta_{1}}{\delta n^{2k}\hat{p}(i+1)^{2}}\leq\frac{\eta_{1}}{\delta^{2}n^{2k}\hat{p}^{2}}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{\eta_{1}}{\delta^{2}H^{2}}\leq\frac{\zeta^{2+2\varepsilon^{3}}\eta_{1}}{\delta^{2}H}\leq\frac{\zeta^{2+\varepsilon^{3}}\eta_{1}}{H}.

Thus we also obtain the desired expression for
\Delta\eta_{1}
.
∎

Lemma A.4
.

Let
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta H^{*}\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}H^{*}}{H}\pm\frac{\zeta^{2}\hat{h}^{*}}{\delta^{5}H}.

Proof.

Fix
f\in\mathcal{F}
.
Lemma
7.19
entails

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta H^{*}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle=_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{1}{H^{*}}\sum_{\mathcal{F}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{H}^{*}}\Big\lparen\Big\lparen\sum_{e\in\mathcal{F}^{\prime}}d_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}(e)\Big\rparen\pm\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}\zeta^{2+\varepsilon^{2}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}\Big\rparen

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{H^{*}}\Big\lparen\sum_{e\in\mathcal{H}}d_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}(e)^{2}\Big\rparen\pm\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}\zeta^{2+\varepsilon^{2}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}.

For all
e\in\mathcal{H}
, from Lemma
7.18
, we obtain

d_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}(e)=_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})}\pm\frac{1}{\delta}\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}.

Thus, Lemma
7.21
yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta H^{*}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle=_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{1}{H^{*}}\frac{\lparen\sum_{e\in\mathcal{H}}d_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}(e)\rparen^{2}}{H}\pm\frac{2\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}(k!)^{2}\zeta^{2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}^{2}H}{\delta^{2}H^{*}}\pm\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}\zeta^{2+\varepsilon^{2}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}H^{*}}{H}\pm\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}H}{H^{*}}\frac{\zeta^{2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}H}{\delta^{3}H}\pm\frac{\zeta^{2+\varepsilon^{2}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}H}{\delta H}.

Since Lemma
7.7
implies
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}H\leq\hat{h}^{*}/\varepsilon
, we obtain

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta H^{*}\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}H^{*}}{H}\pm\frac{\zeta^{2}\hat{h}^{*}}{\delta^{5}H},

which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma A.5
.

Let
0\leq i_{0}\leq i
and
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\mathclose{]}\leq 0
.

Proof.

Suppose that
i<i^{\star}
and let
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tau_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\wedge\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\mathclose{\}}
.
We have
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}^{\mathsf{c}}}0
and
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}
, so it suffices to obtain
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}0
.
Combining Lemma
A.3
with Lemma
A.4
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle={\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta H^{*}\mathclose{]}-\Delta\hat{h}^{*})-\Delta\eta_{1}

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\bigg\lparen-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}}{H}H^{*}+\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}}{H}\hat{h}^{*}\bigg\rparen+\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert-\frac{(1+\varepsilon^{3})\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\eta_{1}+\frac{\zeta^{2}}{\delta^{5}H}\hat{h}^{*}+\frac{2\zeta^{2+\varepsilon^{2}}}{H}\hat{h}^{*}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\bigg\lparen{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert(H^{*}-\hat{h}^{*})-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert-\frac{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\eta_{1}-\varepsilon^{2}\eta_{1}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert(1-\varepsilon)\eta_{1}-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert-\frac{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\eta_{1}-\varepsilon^{2}\eta_{1}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle=-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\eta_{1}}{H}\bigg\lparen\frac{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}-\varepsilon\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert-\varepsilon^{2}\bigg\rparen\leq 0,

which completes the proof.
∎

A.2.
Boundedness

As we intend to apply Azuma’s inequality, it suffices to obtain suitable bounds for the absolute one-step changes of the processes
Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(0),Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(1),\ldots
and
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}(i_{0}),Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}(i_{0}+1),\ldots
.

Lemma A.6
.

Let
0\leq i_{0}\leq i\leq i^{\star}
,
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
,
f\in\mathcal{F}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tau_{\mathscr{F}}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,
\lvert\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}(i_{0})/\delta
.

Proof.

From Lemma
7.18
, we obtain

\lvert\Delta H^{*}\rvert\leq\sum_{e\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(i+1)}d_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}(e)\leq\sum_{e\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(i+1)}\sum_{f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{\psi\colon f^{\prime}\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e}\Phi_{\mathcal{F},\psi}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}2\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}k!\,\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}.

Hence, using Lemma
A.3
, we have

\lvert\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\leq\lvert\Delta H^{*}\rvert+\lvert\Delta\hat{h}^{*}\rvert+\lvert\Delta\eta_{1}\rvert\leq_{\mathcal{X}}2\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}k!\,\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}+\frac{2\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}\hat{h}^{*}}{H}+\frac{2\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}\eta_{1}}{H}.

With Lemma
7.7
and
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}\leq\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}(i_{0})
, this completes the proof.
∎

Lemma A.7
.

Let
0\leq i_{0}\leq i
,
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
and
f\in\mathcal{F}
.
Then,
\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\rvert\leq\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}(i_{0})/\delta
.

Proof.

This is an immediate consequence of Lemma
A.6
.
∎

A.3.
Supermartingale concentration

This section follows a similar structure as Sections
8.3.3
and
9.2.3
.
Lemma
A.8
is the final ingredient that we use for our application of Azuma’s inequality in the proof of Lemma
A.9
where we show that the probabilities of the events on the right in Observation
A.1
are indeed small.

Lemma A.8
.

Let
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})\leq-\varepsilon^{2}\eta_{1}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})
.

Proof.

Lemma
7.4
implies
\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\geq 1
and
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(H^{*}(0)-\hat{h}^{*}(0))<\eta_{0}(0)
, so we have
\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\geq 1
.
Thus, by definition of
\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}
, for
i:=\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}-1
, we have
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(H^{*}-\hat{h}^{*})\leq\eta_{0}
and thus

Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}={\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(H^{*}-\hat{h}^{*})-\eta_{1}\leq-\varepsilon\eta_{1}.

Furthermore, since
\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\leq\tau_{\mathscr{F}}
, we may apply Lemma
A.6
such that with Lemma
7.7
and Lemma
7.9
, for
f\in\mathcal{F}
, we obtain

Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})=Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}+\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\leq-\varepsilon\eta_{1}+\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}}{\delta}\leq-\varepsilon\eta_{1}+\frac{\hat{h}^{*}}{\delta^{2}H}\leq-\varepsilon\eta_{1}+\zeta^{2+\varepsilon^{2}}\hat{h}^{*}\leq-\varepsilon^{2}\eta_{1}.

Since
\Delta\eta_{1}\leq 0
, this completes the proof.
∎

Lemma A.9
.

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-n^{\varepsilon^{2}})
.

Proof.

Fix
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
.
By Observation
A.1
, is suffices to show that

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-n^{2\varepsilon^{2}}).

Due to Lemma
A.8
, we have

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{]}\leq\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(i^{\star})-Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})\geq\varepsilon^{2}\eta_{1}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})\mathclose{]}\leq\sum_{0\leq i\leq i^{\star}}\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(i^{\star})-Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}\geq\varepsilon^{2}\eta_{1}\mathclose{]}.

Thus, for
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
, it suffices to obtain

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(i^{\star})-Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}\geq\varepsilon^{2}\eta_{1}\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-n^{3\varepsilon^{2}}).

We show that this bound is a consequence of Azuma’s inequality.

Fix
f\in\mathcal{F}
.
Lemma
A.4
shows that
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(i),Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(i+1),\ldots
is a supermartingale, while Lemma
A.7
provides the bound
\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(j)\rvert\leq\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}/\delta
for all
j\geq i
.
Hence, we may apply Lemma
13.5
to obtain

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(i^{\star})-Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}\geq\varepsilon^{2}\eta_{1}\mathclose{]}\leq\exp\bigg\lparen-\frac{\varepsilon^{4}\delta^{2}\eta_{1}^{2}}{2(i^{\star}-i)\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}^{2}}\bigg\rparen.

Since

i^{\star}-i\leq\frac{\vartheta n^{k}}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}-i=\frac{n^{k}\hat{p}}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!},

this yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(i^{\star})-Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}\geq\varepsilon^{2}\eta_{1}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle\leq\exp\bigg\lparen-\frac{\varepsilon^{5}\delta^{2}\eta_{1}^{2}}{n^{k}\hat{p}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}^{2}}\bigg\rparen=\exp\bigg\lparen-\frac{\varepsilon^{5}\delta^{2}\zeta^{2+2\varepsilon^{3}}(\hat{h}^{*})^{2}}{n^{k}\hat{p}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}^{2}}\bigg\rparen\leq\exp\lparen-\delta^{3}\zeta^{2+2\varepsilon^{3}}n^{k}\hat{p}\rparen

\displaystyle\leq\exp\lparen-\delta^{3}\zeta^{2+2\varepsilon^{3}}(n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{F}}})^{k}\rparen=\exp\lparen-\delta^{3}n^{2k\varepsilon^{2}}\zeta^{2+2\varepsilon^{3}-2k}\rparen\leq\exp(-n^{4\varepsilon^{2}}),

which completes the proof.
∎

Appendix B
Counting balanced templates

In this section, our goal is to prove Lemma
7.20

(ii)
.
Hence, for this section, we assume the setup that we used in Section
7
to state Lemma
7.20
.
Similarly as in Sections
8.3
and
9.2
, this requires us to consider several balanced templates, however, it again suffices to essentially only consider a fixed balanced template
(\mathcal{A},I)
, see Observation
B.1
below.
Moreover, we may assume that
\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]\neq\emptyset
as otherwise, for all
\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}
and
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
, we have
\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}=(1\pm\zeta^{\delta})\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}
as a consequence of Lemma
7.8
.
Overall, our approach is similar as in Sections
8.3
and
9.2
.

Observation B.1
.

For
(\mathcal{A},I)\in\mathscr{B}
and
\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}
, let

\tau_{\mathcal{A},\psi}:=\min\mathopen{\{}i\geq 0\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\neq(1\pm\zeta^{\delta})\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\mathclose{\}}.

Then,

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\mathcal{A},I)\in\mathscr{B}\colon\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]\neq\emptyset,\\
\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{\delta^{1/2}}_{\mathcal{A},I}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}.

Fix
(\mathcal{A},I)\in\mathscr{B}
with
\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]\neq\emptyset
and
\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}
and for
i\geq 0
, let

\xi_{1}(i):=\zeta^{\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\quad\text{and}\quad\xi_{0}(i):=(1-\delta^{2})\xi_{1}

and define the stopping time

\tau:=\min\mathopen{\{}i\geq 0\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\neq\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\pm\xi_{1}\mathclose{\}}.

We only expect tight concentration of
\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}
around
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}
as long as we expect
\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}
to be sufficiently large, that is up to step
i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}
.
Formally, in this section it is our goal to obtain an upper bound for the probability that
\tau\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\wedge i^{\star}
and hence the minimum
i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\wedge i^{\star}
often plays the role that
i^{\star}
plays in Sections
8.3
and
9.2
.

Define the critical intervals

I^{-}(i):=[\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}-\xi_{1},\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}-\xi_{0}]\quad\text{and}\quad I^{+}(i):=[\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}+\xi_{0},\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}+\xi_{1}].

For
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
, let

Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i):={\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I})-\xi_{1}.

For
i_{0}\geq 0
define the stopping time

\tau^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}:=\min\mathopen{\{}i\geq i_{0}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\notin I^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{\}}

and for
i\geq i_{0}
, let

Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}(i):=Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i_{0}\vee(i\wedge\tau^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\wedge\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{\delta^{1/2}}_{\mathcal{A},I}\wedge i^{\star})).

Let

\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}:=\min\mathopen{\{}j\geq 0\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I})\geq\xi_{0}\text{ for all }j\leq i<\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{\delta^{1/2}}_{\mathcal{A},I}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{\}}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{\delta^{1/2}}_{\mathcal{A},I}\wedge i^{\star}.

With this setup, similarly as in Sections
8.3
and
9.2
, it in fact suffices to consider the evolution of
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}),Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}+1),\ldots
.

Observation B.2
.

\mathopen{\{}\tau\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{\delta^{1/2}}_{\mathcal{A},I}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{\}}\subseteq\mathopen{\{}Z^{-}_{\sigma^{-}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{\}}\cup\mathopen{\{}Z^{+}_{\sigma^{+}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{\}}
.

We again use supermartingale concentration techniques to show that the probabilities of the events on the right in Observation
B.2
are sufficiently small.
More specifically, for this section, we use Lemma
8.20
.

B.1.
Trend

Here, we prove that for all
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
and
i_{0}\geq 0
, the expected one-step changes of the process
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}(i_{0}),Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}(i_{0}+1),\ldots
are non-positive.
Lemma
7.12
already yields estimates for the one-step changes of the relevant deterministic trajectory, in Lemma
B.4
we estimate the one-step changes of the error term that we use in this section.
Then we state Lemma
B.5
