where we provide a precise estimate for the expected one-step change of the non-deterministic part that holds whenever the removal process was well-behaved up to the step we consider.
Finally, combining these estimates shows that the above process is indeed a supermartingale (see Lemma
B.6
).

Observation B.3
.

Extend
\hat{p}
and
\xi_{1}
to continuous trajectories defined on the whole interval
[0,i^{\star}+1]
using the same expressions as above.
Then, for
x\in[0,i^{\star}+1]
,

\begin{gathered}\xi_{1}^{\prime}(x)=-\frac{(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert-\frac{\delta\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2})\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\xi_{1}(x)}{n^{k}\hat{p}(x)},\\
\xi_{1}^{\prime\prime}(x)=-\frac{(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert-\frac{\delta\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2})(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert-\frac{\delta\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}-1)\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}(k!)^{2}\xi_{1}(x)}{n^{2k}\hat{p}(x)^{2}}.\end{gathered}

Lemma B.4
.

Let
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i\leq\tau_{\emptyset}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\Delta\xi_{1}=_{\mathcal{X}}-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert-\frac{\delta\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\xi_{1}}{H}\pm\frac{\zeta\xi_{1}}{H}.

Proof.

This is a consequence of Taylor’s theorem.
In detail, we argue as follows.

Together with Observation
B.3
, Lemma
7.10
yields

\Delta\xi_{1}=-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert-\frac{\delta\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\xi_{1}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}\pm\max_{x\in[i,i+1]}\frac{\xi_{1}(x)}{\delta n^{2k}\hat{p}(x)^{2}}.

We investigate the first term and the maximum separately.
Using Lemma
7.7
, we have

-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert-\frac{\delta\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\xi_{1}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}=_{\mathcal{X}}-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert-\frac{\delta\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\xi_{1}}{H}.

Furthermore, using Lemma
7.6
, Lemma
7.7
and Lemma
7.9
yields

\max_{x\in[i,i+1]}\frac{\xi_{1}(x)}{\delta n^{2k}\hat{p}(x)^{2}}\leq\frac{\xi_{1}}{\delta n^{2k}\hat{p}(i+1)^{2}}\leq\frac{\xi_{1}}{\delta^{2}n^{2k}\hat{p}^{2}}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\delta^{2}H^{2}}\leq\frac{\zeta^{2+2\varepsilon^{2}}\xi_{1}}{\delta^{2}H}\leq\frac{\zeta^{2+\varepsilon^{2}}\xi_{1}}{H}.

Thus we obtain the desired expression for
\Delta\xi_{1}
.
∎

Lemma B.5
.

Let
0\leq i\leq i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\wedge i^{\star}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}}-(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\pm\frac{\zeta^{1/2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{H}.

Proof.

Fix
f\in\mathcal{F}
.
Lemma
7.19
entails

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{1}{H^{*}}\sum_{\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}^{\sim}}\Big\lparen\Big\lparen\sum_{e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]}d_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}(\varphi(e))\Big\rparen\pm\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert^{2}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}\Big\rparen.

From Lemma
7.18
, for all
e\in\mathcal{H}
, we obtain

d_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}(e)=_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})}\pm\frac{1}{\delta}\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}.

Thus, due to Lemma
7.7
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle=_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{1}{H^{*}}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\bigg\lparen(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})}\pm\frac{1}{\delta^{2}}\zeta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle=-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}}{\aut(\mathcal{F})H^{*}}\bigg\lparen(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\pm\frac{1}{\delta^{3}}\zeta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle=_{\mathcal{X}}-(1\pm\zeta^{1+\varepsilon^{4}})\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\bigg\lparen(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\pm\frac{1}{\delta^{3}}\zeta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle=-(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\pm\frac{\zeta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}}{\delta^{4}H}=_{\mathcal{X}}-(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\pm\frac{\zeta^{1/2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{H},

which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma B.6
.

Let
0\leq i_{0}\leq i
and
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
. Then,
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\mathclose{]}\leq 0
.

Proof.

Suppose that
i\leq i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\wedge i^{\star}
and let
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tau^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\wedge\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\mathclose{\}}
.
We have
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}^{\mathsf{c}}}0
and
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}
, so it suffices to obtain
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}0
.
Combining Lemma
7.12
, Lemma
B.4
and Lemma
B.5
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle={\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\mathclose{]}-\Delta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I})-\Delta\xi_{1}

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\bigg\lparen-(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}+(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle\mathrel{}\quad+\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert-\frac{\delta\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\xi_{1}}{H}+\frac{\zeta^{1/3}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{H}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\bigg\lparen{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)(\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I})-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert-\frac{\delta\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\zeta^{\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}-\zeta^{1/3}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{H}\bigg\lparen(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)(1-\delta^{2})\zeta^{\delta}-\bigg\lparen\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert-\frac{\delta\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\bigg\rparen\zeta^{\delta}-\zeta^{1/3}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle=-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{H}\bigg\lparen-(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\delta^{2}\zeta^{\delta}+\frac{\delta\rho_{\mathcal{F}}}{2}\zeta^{\delta}-\zeta^{1/3}\bigg\rparen\leq 0,

which completes the proof.
∎

B.2.
Boundedness

Here, similarly as in Sections
8.3.2
and
9.2.2
, we obtain suitable bounds for the absolute one-step changes of the processes
Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(0),Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(1),\ldots
and
Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i_{0}),Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i_{0}+1),\ldots
(see Lemma
B.7
and Lemma
B.8
) as well as for the expected absolute one-step changes of these processes (see Lemma
B.9
and Lemma
B.10
).
To obtain these bounds, we argue similarly as in Section
8.3
.

Lemma B.7
.

Let
0\leq i_{0}\leq i\leq i^{\delta^{1/2}}_{\mathcal{A},I}\wedge i^{\star}
,
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}^{\prime}}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,
\lvert\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\zeta(i_{0})^{8\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}(i_{0})
.

Proof.

From Lemma
7.12
and Lemma
B.4
, we obtain

\lvert\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\leq\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\rvert+\lvert\Delta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\rvert+\lvert\Delta\xi_{1}\rvert\leq\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\rvert+2\frac{\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{H}+2\frac{\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\xi_{1}}{H}.

Hence, since
\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]\neq\emptyset
implies
\zeta^{8\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq\zeta(i_{0})^{8\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}(i_{0})
, by Lemma
7.9
it suffices to show that

\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\rvert\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\zeta^{9\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I},

which we obtain as a consequence of Lemma
7.17
.

To this end, note that for all
(\mathcal{B},I)\subseteq(\mathcal{A},I)
with
V_{\mathcal{B}}\neq I
, since
(\mathcal{A},I)
is balanced, we have
\rho_{\mathcal{B},I}\leq\rho_{\mathcal{A},I}
and thus using Lemma
7.13
, we obtain

\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{B},I}=(n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{B},I}})^{\lvert V_{\mathcal{B}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert}\geq(n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{A},I}})^{\lvert V_{\mathcal{B}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert}=\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{(\lvert V_{\mathcal{B}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert)/(\lvert V_{\mathcal{A}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert)}\geq\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{10\delta^{1/2}}\geq\frac{\zeta^{-10\delta}}{2}.

Hence, Lemma
7.17
implies

\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\rvert\leq\sum_{e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[{I}]}\lvert\mathopen{\{}\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}^{\sim}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\varphi(e)\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(i+1)\mathclose{\}}\rvert\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert\cdot 4k!\,\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}}\zeta^{10\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq\zeta^{9\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I},

which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma B.8
.

Let
0\leq i_{0}\leq i
and
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,
\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\rvert\leq\zeta(i_{0})^{8\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}(i_{0})
.

Proof.

This is an immediate consequence of Lemma
B.7
.
∎

Lemma B.9
.

Let
0\leq i\leq i^{\delta^{1/2}}_{\mathcal{A},I}\wedge i^{\star}
,
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{\zeta^{5\delta}n^{k}\hat{p}}.

Proof.

From Lemma
7.12
and Lemma
B.4
, we obtain

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}\rvert\mathclose{]}+\lvert\Delta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\rvert+\lvert\Delta\xi_{1}\rvert\leq\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}\rvert\mathclose{]}+2\frac{\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{H}+2\frac{\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\xi_{1}}{H}.

Hence, since
\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]\neq\emptyset
implies

\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{\zeta^{5\delta}n^{k}\hat{p}}\leq\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}(i_{0})}{\zeta(i_{0})^{5\delta}n^{k}\hat{p}(i_{0})},

by Lemma
7.7
implies that it suffices to show that

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{\zeta^{4\delta}n^{k}\hat{p}}.

We obtain this as a consequence of Lemma
7.15
and Lemma
7.17
.

We argue similarly as in the proof of Lemma
8.29
.
For
e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]
, from all subtemplates
(\mathcal{B},I)\subseteq(\mathcal{A},I)
with
e\in\mathcal{B}
choose
(\mathcal{B}_{e},I)
such that
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{B}_{e},I}
is minimal.
Furthermore, for every subtemplate
(\mathcal{B},I)\subseteq(\mathcal{A},I)
, let

\Phi^{e}_{\mathcal{B},\psi}:=\lvert\mathopen{\{}\varphi\in\Phi^{\sim}_{\mathcal{B},\psi}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\varphi(e)\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(i+1)\mathclose{\}}\rvert.

Lemma
7.17
yields

\Phi^{e}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}2k!\,\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I\cup e}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{B}_{e},I}},

so we obtain

(B.1)

\displaystyle\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]}\Phi^{e}_{\mathcal{A},\psi}=\sum_{e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathopen{\{}\Phi^{e}_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\geq 1\mathclose{\}}}\Phi^{e}_{\mathcal{A},\psi}

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}2k!\,\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I\cup e}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\sum_{e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]}\frac{\mathbbm{1}_{\mathopen{\{}\Phi^{e}_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\geq 1\mathclose{\}}}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{B}_{e},I}}\leq\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{\zeta^{\delta}}\sum_{e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]}\frac{\mathbbm{1}_{\mathopen{\{}\Phi^{e}_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\geq 1\mathclose{\}}}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{B}_{e},I}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{\zeta^{\delta}}\sum_{e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]}\frac{\mathbbm{1}_{\mathopen{\{}\Phi^{e}_{\mathcal{B}_{e},\psi}\geq 1\mathclose{\}}}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{B}_{e},I}}\leq\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{\zeta^{\delta}}\sum_{e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]}\sum_{\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathcal{B}_{e},\psi}^{\sim}}\frac{\mathbbm{1}_{\mathopen{\{}\varphi(e)\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(i+1)\mathclose{\}}}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{B}_{e},I}}.

For all
e\in\mathcal{H}
,
f\in\mathcal{F}
and
\psi^{\prime}\colon f\mathchoice{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}{\xrightarrow{\smash{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\sim$}}}}e
, we have
\Phi_{\mathcal{F},\psi^{\prime}}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm\delta^{-1}\zeta)\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}
.
Furthermore, we have
H^{*}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm\zeta^{1+\varepsilon^{3}})\hat{h}^{*}
.
Thus, using Lemma
7.18
, for all
e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]
and
\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathcal{B}_{e},\psi}^{\sim}
, we obtain

\mathbb{P}_{i}\mathopen{[}\varphi(e)\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(i+1)\mathclose{]}=\frac{d_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}(\varphi(e))}{H^{*}}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{2\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}}{H^{*}}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{4\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}}{\hat{h}^{*}}\leq\frac{1}{\zeta^{\delta}n^{k}\hat{p}}.

Combining this with (
B.1
) yields

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{\zeta^{\delta}}\sum_{e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]}\sum_{\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathcal{B}_{e},I}^{\sim}}\frac{\mathbb{P}_{i}\mathopen{[}\varphi(e)\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(i+1)\mathclose{]}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{B}_{e},I}}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{\zeta^{2\delta}n^{k}\hat{p}}\sum_{e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]}\frac{\Phi_{\mathcal{B}_{e},I}}{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{B}_{e},I}}.

This shows that it suffices to prove that
\Phi_{\mathcal{B}_{e},I}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{B}_{e},I}/\zeta^{\delta}
, which we obtain as a consequence of Lemma
7.15
as follows.
First, note that since
(\mathcal{A},I)
is balanced, for all
e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]
and
(\mathcal{C},I)\subseteq(\mathcal{B}_{e},I)\subseteq(\mathcal{A},I)
, we have
\rho_{\mathcal{C},I}\leq\rho_{\mathcal{A},I}
and thus

\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{C},I}=(n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{C},I}})^{\lvert V_{\mathcal{C}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert}\geq(n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{A},I}})^{\lvert V_{\mathcal{C}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert}=\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{(\lvert V_{\mathcal{C}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert)/(\lvert V_{\mathcal{A}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert)}.

As Lemma
7.13
implies
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\geq(1-n^{-\varepsilon^{3}})\zeta^{-\delta^{1/2}}\geq 1
, this entails
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{C},I}\geq 1
and so Lemma
7.15
indeed yields

\Phi_{\mathcal{B}_{e},I}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}2(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{B}_{e},I}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{B}_{e},I}\leq\frac{1}{\zeta^{\delta}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{B}_{e},I},

which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma B.10
.

Let
0\leq i_{0}\leq i^{\star}
and
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,
\sum_{i\geq i_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}(i_{0})/\zeta(i_{0})^{5\delta}
.

Proof.

Lemma
B.9
entails

\sum_{i\geq i_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\rvert\mathclose{]}=\sum_{i_{0}\leq i\leq i^{\star}-1}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq(i^{\star}-i_{0})\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}(i_{0})}{\zeta(i_{0})^{5\delta}n^{k}\hat{p}(i_{0})}.

Since

i^{\star}-i_{0}\leq\frac{\vartheta n^{k}}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}-i_{0}=\frac{n^{k}\hat{p}(i_{0})}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}\leq n^{k}\hat{p}(i_{0}),

this completes the proof.
∎

B.3.
Supermartingale concentration

This section follows a similar structure as Sections
8.3.3
and
9.2.3
.
Lemma
B.11
is the final ingredient that we use for our application of Lemma
8.20
in the proof of Lemma
B.12
where we show that the probabilities of the events on the right in Observation
B.2
are indeed small.

Lemma B.11
.

Let
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})\leq-\delta^{3}\xi_{1}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})
.

Proof.

Lemma
7.4
implies
\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\geq 1
and we have
i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\geq 1
.
Hence, we have
\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\wedge i^{\star}\geq 1
and since for
i:=0
, Lemma
7.4
also implies
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I})\leq\xi_{0}
, we have
\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\geq 1
.
Thus, by definition of
\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}
, for
i:=\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}-1
, we have
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I})\leq\xi_{0}
and thus

Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}={\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I})-\xi_{1}\leq-\delta^{2}\xi_{1}.

Furthermore, since
\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\leq\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}^{\prime}}
, we may apply Lemma
B.7
to obtain

Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})=Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}+\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\leq Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}+\zeta^{8\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq-\delta^{2}\xi_{1}+\zeta^{8\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq-\delta^{3}\xi_{1}.

Since
\Delta\xi_{1}\leq 0
, this completes the proof.
∎

Lemma B.12
.

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-n^{\delta^{2}})
.

Proof.

Considering Observation
B.1
, it suffices to show that

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{\delta^{1/2}}_{\mathcal{A},I}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-n^{2\delta^{2}}).

Hence, by Observation
B.2
, it suffices to show that for
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
, we have

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-n^{3\delta^{2}}).

Due to Lemma
B.11
, we have

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{]}\leq\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(i^{\star})-Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})\geq\delta^{3}\xi_{1}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})\mathclose{]}\leq\sum_{0\leq i\leq i^{\star}}\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(i^{\star})-Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}\geq\delta^{3}\xi_{1}\mathclose{]}.

Thus, for
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
, it suffices to obtain

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(i^{\star})-Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}\geq\delta^{3}\xi_{1}\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-n^{4\delta^{2}}).

We show that this bound is a consequence of Lemma
8.20
.

Let us turn to the details.
Lemma
B.6
shows that
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(i),Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(i+1),\ldots
is a supermartingale,
while Lemma
B.8
provides the bound
\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(j)\rvert\leq\zeta^{8\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}
for all
j\geq i
and Lemma
B.10
provides the bound
\sum_{j\geq i}\mathbb{E}_{j}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(j)\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq\zeta^{-5\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}
.
Hence, we may apply Lemma
8.20
such that using Lemma
7.8
, we obtain

\mathbb{P}_{i}\mathopen{[}Z_{i}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i^{\star})-Z_{i}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}>\delta^{3}\xi_{1}\mathclose{]}\leq\exp\bigg\lparen-\frac{\delta^{6}\zeta^{2\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{2}}{2\zeta^{8\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}(\delta^{3}\zeta^{\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}+\zeta^{-5\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I})}\bigg\rparen\leq\exp(\delta^{7}\zeta^{-\delta})\leq\exp(-n^{4\delta^{2}}),

which completes the proof.
∎

Appendix C
Counting strictly balanced templates

Lemma
7.20

(ii)
states that for a balanced template
(\mathcal{A},I)\in\mathscr{B}
and
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
, the number
\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}
behaves as expected as long as the corresponding trajectory still suggests a significant number of embeddings in the sense that
i\leq i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}
.
In this section, our goal is to extend this guarantee that the number of embeddings is typically concentrated around the trajectory also beyond step
i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}
up to step
i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{0}
and also if
i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}=0
subject to the following two restrictions.
First, we obtain this guarantee only for strictly balanced templates
(\mathcal{A},I)
with
i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{0}\geq 1
and second, we allow larger relative deviations from the trajectory compared to Lemma
7.20

(ii)
.
Formally, for this section, we assume the setup that we used in Section
7
to state Lemma
7.20
and show that the probability that
\tau_{\mathscr{B}^{\prime}}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}
is small.
Similarly as in Sections
8.3
and
9.2
we may again restrict our attention to only one fixed strictly balanced template
(\mathcal{A},I)
with
I\neq V_{\mathcal{A}}
and
i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\leq i^{\star}
, see Observation
C.1
.
Note that
I\neq V_{\mathcal{A}}
together with
i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\leq i^{\star}
in particular entails
\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]\neq\emptyset
.
Overall, our approach is similar as in Sections
8.3
and
9.2
, however, the fact that here we are only interested in steps
i\geq i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}
leads to a slightly different setup where we intuitively shift the beginning of our considerations from step
0
to step
i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}
.
To control the initial situation at this shifted start, we rely on Lemma
7.20

(ii)
.

Observation C.1
.

For
(\mathcal{A},I)\in\mathscr{B}^{\prime}
and
\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}
, let

\tau_{\mathcal{A},\psi}:=\min\mathopen{\{}i\geq i^{\delta^{1/2}}_{\mathcal{A},I}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\neq(1\pm(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{-\delta^{1/2}})\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\mathclose{\}}.

Then,

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau_{\mathscr{B}^{\prime}}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\mathcal{A},I)\in\mathscr{B}^{\prime}\colon I\neq V_{\mathcal{A}}\text{ and }i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\leq i^{\star}\\
\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{0}_{\mathcal{A},I}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}.

Fix
(\mathcal{A},I)\in\mathscr{B}^{\prime}
with
I\neq V_{\mathcal{A}}
and
i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\leq i^{\star}
and hence
\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]\neq\emptyset
.
Let
\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow V_{\mathcal{H}}
and for
i\geq 0
, let

\xi_{1}(i):=(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{1-\delta^{1/2}},\quad\xi_{0}(i):=(1-\delta)\xi_{1}

and define the stopping time

\tau:=\min\mathopen{\{}i\geq i^{\delta^{1/2}}_{\mathcal{A},I}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\neq\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\pm\xi_{1}\mathclose{\}}.

Define the critical intervals

I^{-}(i):=[\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}-\xi_{1},\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}-\xi_{0}],\quad I^{+}(i):=[\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}+\xi_{0},\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}+\xi_{1}].

For
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
, let

Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i):={\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I})-\xi_{1}.

For
i_{0}\geq i^{\delta^{1/2}}_{\mathcal{A},I}
, define the stopping time

\tau^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}:=\min\mathopen{\{}i\geq i_{0}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\notin I^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{\}}

and for
i\geq i_{0}
, let

Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}(i):=\mathbbm{1}_{\mathopen{\{}i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}<\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\mathclose{\}}}Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(i_{0}\vee(i\wedge\tau^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\wedge\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{0}_{\mathcal{A},I}\wedge i^{\star})).

Let

\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}:=\min\mathopen{\{}j\geq i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I})\geq\xi_{0}\text{ for all }j\leq i<\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{0}_{\mathcal{A},I}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{\}}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{0}_{\mathcal{A},I}\wedge i^{\star}.

With this setup, similarly as in Section
8.3
and Section
9.2
, it in fact again suffices to consider the evolution of
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}),Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}+1),\ldots
.
Indeed, we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{\{}\tau\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{0}_{\mathcal{A},I}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{\}}

\displaystyle\subseteq\mathopen{\{}\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\leq i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\text{ and }\tau\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{0}_{\mathcal{A},I}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{\}}\cup\mathopen{\{}Z^{-}_{\sigma^{-}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{\}}\cup\mathopen{\{}Z^{+}_{\sigma^{+}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{\}}

\displaystyle\subseteq\mathopen{\{}i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\leq\tau\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\leq\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\leq i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\mathclose{\}}\cup\mathopen{\{}Z^{-}_{\sigma^{-}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{\}}\cup\mathopen{\{}Z^{+}_{\sigma^{+}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{\}}

\displaystyle\subseteq\mathopen{\{}\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\mathclose{\}}\cup\mathopen{\{}Z^{-}_{\sigma^{-}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{\}}\cup\mathopen{\{}Z^{+}_{\sigma^{+}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{\}}

and due to
i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\leq i^{\star}
, this leads to the following observation.

Observation C.2
.

\mathopen{\{}\tau\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{0}_{\mathcal{A},I}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{\}}\subseteq\mathopen{\{}\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{\}}\cup\mathopen{\{}Z^{-}_{\sigma^{-}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{\}}\cup\mathopen{\{}Z^{+}_{\sigma^{+}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{\}}
.

Lemma
7.20

(ii)
shows that the probability of the first event on the right in Observation
C.2
is sufficiently small and we again use supermartingale concentration techniques to show that the probabilities of the other two events are also sufficiently small.
More specifically, for this section, we use Lemma
8.20
.

C.1.
Trend

Here, we prove that for all
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
and
i_{0}\geq i^{\delta^{1/2}}_{\mathcal{A},I}
, the expected one-step changes of the process
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}(i_{0}),Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}(i_{0}+1),\ldots
are non-positive.
Lemma
7.12
already yields estimates for the one-step changes of the relevant deterministic trajectory, in Lemma
C.4
we estimate the one-step changes of the error term that we use in this section.
Furthermore, Lemma
B.5
provides a precise estimate for the expected one-step change of the non-deterministic part of the random process.
Combining these estimates shows that the process indeed is a supermartingale (see Lemma
C.5
).

Observation C.3
.

Extend
\hat{p}
and
\xi_{1}
to continuous trajectories defined on the whole interval
[0,i^{\star}+1]
using the same expressions as above.
Then, for
x\in[0,i^{\star}+1]
,

\begin{gathered}\xi_{1}^{\prime}(x)=-\frac{(1-\delta^{1/2})(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\xi_{1}(x)}{n^{k}\hat{p}(x)},\\
\xi_{1}^{\prime\prime}(x)=-\frac{(1-\delta^{1/2})(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)((1-\delta^{1/2})(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)-1)\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert^{2}(k!)^{2}\xi_{1}(x)}{n^{2k}\hat{p}(x)^{2}},\end{gathered}

Lemma C.4
.

Let
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i\leq\tau_{\emptyset}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\Delta\xi_{1}=_{\mathcal{X}}-(1-\delta^{1/2})(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\xi_{1}}{H}\pm\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{1-\delta^{1/2}}}{H}.

Proof.

This is a consequence of Taylor’s theorem.
In detail, we argue as follows.

Together with Observation
C.3
, Lemma
7.10
yields

\Delta\xi_{1}=-\frac{(1-\delta^{1/2})(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\xi_{1}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}\pm\max_{x\in[i,i+1]}\frac{\xi_{1}(x)}{\delta n^{2k}\hat{p}(x)^{2}}.

We investigate the first term and the maximum separately.
Using Lemma
7.7
, we have

-\frac{(1-\delta^{1/2})(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\xi_{1}}{n^{k}\hat{p}}=_{\mathcal{X}}-(1-\delta^{1/2})(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\xi_{1}}{H}.

Furthermore, precisely as at the end of the proof of Lemma
B.4
, we obtain

\max_{x\in[i,i+1]}\frac{\xi_{1}(x)}{\delta n^{2k}\hat{p}(x)^{2}}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{\zeta^{2+\varepsilon^{2}}\xi_{1}}{H}.

With Lemma
7.8
, this completes the proof.
∎

Lemma C.5
.

Let
i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\leq i_{0}\leq i
and
