{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\mathclose{]}\leq 0
.

Proof.

Suppose that
i\leq i^{\star}
and let
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tau^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\wedge\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\mathclose{\}}
.
We have
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}^{\mathsf{c}}}0
and
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Z_{i_{0}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}
, so it suffices to obtain
\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}0
.
Due to Lemma
7.8
, we have
\zeta^{1/2}\leq n^{-\delta^{1/2}\lvert V_{\mathcal{A}}\rvert}\leq\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{-\delta^{1/2}}
.
Hence, Lemma
7.12
yields (with room to spare)

\Delta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}=-(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{H}\pm\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{1-\delta^{1/2}}}{H}.

Arguing precisely as in the proof of Lemma
B.5
for the first equality, we obtain

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\mathclose{]}=_{\mathcal{X}}-(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\pm\frac{\zeta^{1/2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{H}=-(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\pm\frac{\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{1-\delta^{1/2}}}{H}.

Combining these two estimates with Lemma
C.4
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle={\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\mathclose{]}-\Delta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I})-\Delta\xi_{1}

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\bigg\lparen-(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}+(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{H}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle\mathrel{}\quad+(1-\delta^{1/2})(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\xi_{1}}{H}+\frac{3\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{1-\delta^{1/2}}}{H}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)}{H}({\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I})-(1-\delta^{1/2})\xi_{1}-3\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{1-\delta^{1/2}})

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}-\frac{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)\xi_{1}}{H}((1-\delta)-(1-\delta^{1/2})-\delta)\leq 0,

which completes the proof.
∎

C.2.
Boundedness

Here, similarly as in Sections
8.3.2
and
9.2.2
, we obtain suitable bounds for the absolute one-step changes of the processes
Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(0),Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(1),\ldots
and
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}(i_{0}),Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}(i_{0}+1),\ldots
(see Lemma
C.7
and Lemma
C.8
) as well as the expected absolute one-step changes of these processes (see Lemma
C.9
and Lemma
C.10
).
The fact that we analyze the evolution potentially even until
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}
is essentially
1
often plays an important role in this section.
Furthermore, we crucially exploit that
(\mathcal{A},I)
is strictly balanced and not just balanced.

Lemma C.6
.

Let
i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\leq i\leq i^{\star}
.
Fix
e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]
and
(\mathcal{B},I)\subseteq(\mathcal{A},I)
with
e\in\mathcal{B}
. Then,
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{B},I}
.

Proof.

If
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq 1
, then

\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{(\lvert V_{\mathcal{B}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert)/(\lvert V_{\mathcal{A}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert)}=(n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{A},I}})^{\lvert V_{\mathcal{B}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert}\leq(n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{B},I}})^{\lvert V_{\mathcal{B}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert}=\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{B},I}.

Hence, we may assume
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\geq 1
.
Furthermore, we may assume that
\mathcal{B}\neq\mathcal{A}
.

Since
(\mathcal{A},I)
is strictly balanced, we have
\rho_{\mathcal{B},I}+\delta^{1/4}\leq\rho_{\mathcal{A},I}
.
This allows us to obtain

\displaystyle\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}

\displaystyle=(n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{A},I}})^{\lvert V_{\mathcal{B}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert}(n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{A},I}})^{\lvert V_{\mathcal{A}}\rvert-\lvert V_{\mathcal{B}}\rvert}=(n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{A},I}})^{\lvert V_{\mathcal{B}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{(\lvert V_{\mathcal{A}}\rvert-\lvert V_{\mathcal{B}}\rvert)/(\lvert V_{\mathcal{A}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert)}

\displaystyle\leq(n\hat{p}^{\rho_{\mathcal{B},I}+\delta^{1/4}})^{\lvert V_{\mathcal{B}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{B},I}\cdot\hat{p}^{\delta^{1/4}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}.

Hence, it suffices to show that
\hat{p}^{\delta^{1/4}}\leq 1/\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}
.
Indeed, using Lemma
7.8
and the fact that
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq\zeta^{-\delta^{1/2}}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\hat{p}^{\delta^{1/4}}

\displaystyle\leq\hat{p}^{\delta^{1/3}(\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert-\lvert\mathcal{A}[I]\rvert)}=n^{-\delta^{1/3}(\lvert V_{\mathcal{A}}\rvert-\lvert I\rvert)}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/3}}\leq n^{-\delta^{1/3}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq\zeta^{\delta^{1/3}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}=(\zeta^{\delta^{1/2}})^{\delta^{-1/6}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}

\displaystyle\leq\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{1-\delta^{-1/6}}\leq\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{-1},

which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma C.7
.

Let
i^{\delta^{1/2}}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq i\leq i^{\star}
,
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}^{\prime}}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\lvert\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}}{\delta^{2}\log n}.

Proof.

From Lemma
7.12
and Lemma
C.4
, using the fact that
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq\zeta^{-\delta^{1/2}}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\lvert\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\rvert+\lvert\Delta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\rvert+\lvert\Delta\xi_{1}\rvert\leq\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\rvert+2\frac{\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{H}+2\frac{\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\xi_{1}}{H}

\displaystyle\leq\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\rvert+2\frac{\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\zeta^{-\delta^{1/2}}}{H}+2\frac{\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}}\zeta^{-\delta^{1/2}(1-\delta^{1/2})}}{H}

\displaystyle\leq\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\rvert+3\frac{\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert}{\zeta^{\delta^{1/2}}H}.

Hence, Lemma
7.9
implies that it suffices to show that

\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\rvert\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}}{\delta\log n}

which we obtain as a consequence of Lemma
7.17
and Lemma
C.6
.
Indeed, these two lemmas together with Observation
7.1
imply

\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\rvert\leq\sum_{e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[{I}]}\lvert\mathopen{\{}\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}^{\sim}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\varphi(e)\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(i+1)\mathclose{\}}\rvert\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert\cdot 2k!\,\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I\cup e}}\leq\frac{(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}}{\delta\log n},

which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma C.8
.

Let
i^{\delta^{1/2}}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq i_{0}\leq i
and
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\rvert\leq\frac{(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}}{\delta^{2}\log n}.

Proof.

This is an immediate consequence of Lemma
C.7
.
∎

Lemma C.9
.

Let
i^{\delta^{1/2}}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq i_{0}\leq i\leq i^{0}_{\mathcal{A},I}\wedge i^{\star}
,
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i<\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{(\log n)^{3\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}(i_{0})}{\delta^{5}n^{k}\hat{p}(i_{0})\log n}.

Proof.

From Lemma
7.12
and Lemma
C.4
, we obtain

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}\rvert\mathclose{]}+\lvert\Delta\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\rvert+\lvert\Delta\xi_{1}\rvert\leq\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}\rvert\mathclose{]}+2\frac{\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{H}+2\frac{\lvert\mathcal{A}\rvert\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert\xi_{1}}{H}.

Since
\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]\neq\emptyset
implies
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}/\hat{p}\leq\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}(i_{0})/\hat{p}(i_{0})
, due to Lemma
7.13
and Lemma
7.7
, it suffices to show that

\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq\frac{(\log n)^{3\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{\delta^{4}n^{k}\hat{p}\log n}.

Arguing similarly as in the proof of
8.29
, we obtain this as a consequence of Lemma
7.17
and Lemma
C.6
.

To this end, for
e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]
, let

\Phi^{e}_{\mathcal{A},I}:=\lvert\mathopen{\{}\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\sim}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\varphi(e)\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(i+1)\mathclose{\}}\rvert.

Using Observation
7.1
, Lemma
7.17
together with Lemma
C.6
yields

\Phi^{e}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}2k!\,\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I\cup e}}\leq\frac{(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}}{\delta\log n},

so we obtain

(C.1)

\displaystyle\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}^{e}=\sum_{e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathopen{\{}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}^{e}\geq 1\mathclose{\}}}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}^{e}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}}{\delta\log n}\sum_{e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathopen{\{}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}^{e}\geq 1\mathclose{\}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}}{\delta\log n}\sum_{e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]}\sum_{\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\sim}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathopen{\{}\varphi(e)\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(i+1)\mathclose{\}}}.

For all
e\in\mathcal{H}
,
f\in\mathcal{F}
and
\psi^{\prime}\colon f\hookrightarrow e
, we have
\Phi_{\mathcal{F},\psi^{\prime}}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm\delta^{-1}\zeta)\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}
.
Furthermore, we have
H^{*}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm\zeta^{1+\varepsilon^{3}})\hat{h}^{*}
.
Thus, using Lemma
7.18
, for all
e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]
and
\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\sim}
, we obtain

\mathbb{P}_{i}\mathopen{[}\varphi(e)\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(i+1)\mathclose{]}=\frac{d_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}(\varphi(e))}{H^{*}}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{2\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}}{H^{*}}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{4\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!\,\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{F},f}}{\hat{h}^{*}}\leq\frac{1}{\delta n^{k}\hat{p}}.

Combining this with (
C.1
) and using the fact that
\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}=_{\mathcal{X}}(1\pm(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{-\delta^{1/2}})\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}
as well as Lemma
7.13
yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}\rvert\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}}{\delta^{2}n^{k}\hat{p}\log n}\sum_{e\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\mathcal{A}[I]}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq\frac{(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}}{\delta^{3}n^{k}\hat{p}\log n}\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}

\displaystyle\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\frac{(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}}{\delta^{3}n^{k}\hat{p}\log n}(1+(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{-\delta^{1/2}})\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq\frac{(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}}{\delta^{3}n^{k}\hat{p}\log n}(1+2(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}})\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(\log n)^{3\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{\delta^{4}n^{k}\hat{p}\log n},

which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma C.10
.

Let
i^{\delta^{1/2}}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq i_{0}\leq i^{\star}
and
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,

\sum_{i\geq i_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq\frac{(\log n)^{3\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}(i_{0})}{\delta^{5}\log n}.

Proof.

Lemma
C.9
entails

\sum_{i\geq i_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\rvert\mathclose{]}=\sum_{i_{0}\leq i\leq i^{\star}-1}\mathbb{E}_{i}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i_{0}}\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq(i^{\star}-i_{0})\frac{(\log n)^{3\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}(i_{0})}{\delta^{5}n^{k}\hat{p}(i_{0})\log n}.

Since

i^{\star}-i_{0}\leq\frac{\vartheta n^{k}}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}-i_{0}=\frac{n^{k}\hat{p}(i_{0})}{\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert k!}\leq n^{k}\hat{p}(i_{0}),

this completes the proof.
∎

C.3.
Supermartingale concentration

This section follows a similar structure as Sections
8.3.3
and
9.2.3
.
Lemma
C.11
is the final ingredient that we use for our application of Lemma
8.20
in the proof of Lemma
C.12
where we show that the probabilities of the events on the right in Observation
C.2
are indeed small.
One notable difference compared to the aforementioned sections is the fact that here, our analysis does not start at step
0
but instead at step
i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}
.

Lemma C.11
.

Let
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
and
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}<\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then,
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})\leq_{\mathcal{X}}-\delta^{2}\xi_{1}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})
.

Proof.

If
i=i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}=0
, then Lemma
7.4
implies
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I})\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\xi_{0}
.
If
i=i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\geq 1
, then due to
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq\zeta^{-\delta^{1/2}}
, we have

{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\Phi_{\mathcal{A},I}-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I})\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\zeta^{\delta}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{1-\delta^{1/2}}\leq\xi_{0}

Hence, if
\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}=i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}
, then
Z_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\leq_{\mathcal{X}}\xi_{0}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})-\xi_{1}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})=-\delta\xi_{1}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})
, so we may assume
\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\geq i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}+1
.
Then, by definition of
\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}
, for
i:=\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}-1
, we have
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I})\leq\xi_{0}

and thus

Z_{i}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}={\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}-\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I})-\xi_{1}\leq-\delta\xi_{1}.

Furthermore, since
\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\leq\tau_{\mathscr{B}}\wedge\tau_{\mathscr{B}^{\prime}}\wedge i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{0}
, we may apply Lemma
C.7
and Lemma
7.13
to obtain

Z_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})=Z_{i}^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}+\Delta Y^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\leq Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}+\frac{(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}}{\delta^{2}\log n}\leq-\delta\xi_{1}+\frac{2(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{1-\delta^{1/2}}}{\delta^{2}\log n}\leq-\delta^{2}\xi_{1}.

Since
\Delta\xi_{1}\leq 0
, this completes the proof.
∎

Lemma C.12
.

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau_{\mathscr{B}^{\prime}}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-(\log n)^{3/2})
.

Proof.

Considering Observation
C.1
, it suffices to obtain

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}\tau\leq\tilde{\tau}^{\star}\wedge i^{0}_{\mathcal{A},I}\wedge i^{\star}\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-(\log n)^{5/3}).

Hence, by Observation
C.2
and Lemma
7.20

(ii)
, it suffices to show that for
{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}\in\mathopen{\{}-,+\mathclose{\}}
, we have

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-(\log n)^{7/4}).

Using Lemma
C.11
, we obtain

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(i^{\star})>0\mathclose{]}\leq\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(i^{\star})-Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})\geq\delta^{2}\xi_{1}(\sigma^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}})\mathclose{]}\leq\sum_{i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\leq i\leq i^{\star}}\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(i^{\star})-Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}\geq\delta^{2}\xi_{1}\mathclose{]}.

Thus, for
i_{\mathcal{A},I}^{\delta^{1/2}}\leq i\leq i^{\star}
, it suffices to obtain

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(i^{\star})-Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}\geq\delta^{2}\xi_{1}\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-(\log n)^{9/5}).

We show that this bound is a consequence of Lemma
8.20
.

Lemma
C.5
shows that
Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(i),Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(i+1),\ldots
is a supermartingale, while Lemma
C.8
provides the bound

\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(j)\rvert\leq\frac{(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}}{\delta^{2}\log n}

for all
j\geq i
and Lemma
C.10
provides the bound

\sum_{j\geq 0}\mathbb{E}_{j}\mathopen{[}\lvert\Delta Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(j)\rvert\mathclose{]}\leq\frac{(\log n)^{3\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{\delta^{5}\log n}.

Observe that due to Lemma
7.13
, we have

\frac{(\log n)^{3\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{\delta^{5}\log n}+\delta^{2}\xi_{1}\leq\frac{(\log n)^{3\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{\delta^{5}\log n}+(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}\leq\frac{(\log n)^{3\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}{\delta^{6}\log n}.

Hence, we may apply Lemma
8.20
to obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}(i^{\star})-Z^{\mathbin{\mathchoice{\phantom{\displaystyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\displaystyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\textstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\textstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}{\phantom{\scriptscriptstyle-}\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\smash{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle-$\cr\rotatebox[origin={c}]{45.0}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}\cr}}}}}}}_{i}\geq\delta^{2}\xi_{1}\mathclose{]}

\displaystyle\leq\exp\bigg\lparen-\frac{\delta^{4}(\log n)^{2\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{2-2\delta^{1/2}}}{2\delta^{-2}(\log n)^{\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2-1}\cdot\delta^{-6}(\log n)^{3\alpha_{\mathcal{A},I}/2-1}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}}\bigg\rparen

\displaystyle=\exp\lparen-\delta^{13}(\log n)^{2}\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{1-2\delta^{1/2}}\rparen.

Another application of Lemma
7.13
shows that
\hat{\varphi}_{\mathcal{A},I}^{1-2\delta^{1/2}}\geq 1/2
and hence completes the proof.
∎

Appendix D
Cherries

In this section, we prove Theorems
1.6
and
1.7
.
We argue similarly as for Theorem
1.3
and
1.5
in the sense that we obtain Theorem
1.7
as a consequence of Theorem
D.1
below which plays a similar role as Theorem
13.1
and which we then apply together with Theorem
5.1
to obtain Theorem
1.6
, see Section
D.4
.
To state Theorem
D.1
, we assume the setup described in Section
11
and again consider the
\mathcal{F}
-removal process formally given by Algorithm
5.1
as in Section
13
.
In particular, we define
\mathcal{F}_{0}(i)
,
\mathcal{H}(i)
,
H(i)
,
\mathcal{H}^{*}(i)
and
H^{*}(i)
for
i\geq 0
as well as
\tau_{\emptyset}
as in Section
13
.
Furthermore, we introduce the following terminology.
For a
k
-graph
\mathcal{A}
and
1\leq k^{\prime}\leq k-1
, we say that
\mathcal{A}
is a
k^{\prime}
-cherry
if
\mathcal{A}
has no isolated vertices and exactly two edges such that the two edges of
\mathcal{A}
share
k^{\prime}
vertices.
We say that
\mathcal{A}
is a
cherry
if
\mathcal{A}
is a
k^{\prime}
-cherry for some
1\leq k^{\prime}\leq k-1
.

Theorem D.1
.

If
\mathcal{F}
is a cherry, then
\mathbb{P}\mathopen{[}H(\tau_{\emptyset})\leq n^{k-1/\rho_{\mathcal{F}}-\varepsilon}\mathclose{]}\leq\exp(-n^{1/4})
.

D.1.
Unions of cherries

To prove Theorem
D.1
, we argue similarly as for Theorem
13.1
.
However, some of the key results in Section
12
only hold for hypergraphs with at least three edges since self-avoiding cyclic walks of cherries can form stars, that is hypergraphs where the intersection of any distinct edges is the same vertex set.
This forces us to slightly adapt the corresponding arguments for the cherry case.
More specifically, we employ the following two results that replace Lemma
12.5
and Lemma
12.9
.

For
\ell\geq 2
, we say that a sequence
e_{1},\ldots,e_{\ell}
of distinct
k
-sets forms a
k^{\prime}
-tight self-avoiding cyclic walk
if there exist distinct
k^{\prime}
-sets
U_{1},\ldots,U_{\ell}
with
U_{i}\subseteq e_{i}\cap e_{i+1}
for all
1\leq i\leq\ell
with indices taken modulo
\ell
.
Note that the
k
-graph
\mathcal{S}
with no isolated vertices and edge set
\mathopen{\{}e_{1},\ldots,e_{\ell}\mathclose{\}}
is a union of cherries.
Indeed, for
1\leq i\leq\ell
, the
k
-graph
\mathcal{A}_{i}
with no isolated vertices and edge set
\mathopen{\{}e_{i},e_{i+1}\mathclose{\}}
with indices taken modulo
\ell
is a
k^{\prime\prime}
-cherry for some
k^{\prime\prime}\geq k^{\prime}
and we have
\mathcal{S}=\mathcal{A}_{1}+\ldots+\mathcal{A}_{\ell}
.
Furthermore, the
k
-graphs
\mathcal{A}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{A}_{\ell}
form a self-avoiding cyclic walk as defined in Section
12
.

Lemma D.2
.

Let
1\leq k^{\prime}\leq k-1
.
Suppose that
e_{1},\ldots,e_{\ell}
forms a
k^{\prime}
-tight self-avoiding cyclic walk and let
\mathcal{S}
denote the
k
-graph without isolated vertices and edge set
\mathopen{\{}e_{1},\ldots,e_{\ell}\mathclose{\}}
.
Then, there exists
e\in\mathcal{S}
such that
\rho_{\mathcal{S},e}>1/(k-k^{\prime})
.

Proof.

For
0\leq i\leq\ell
, let
V_{i}:=e_{1}\cup\ldots\cup e_{i}
and for
1\leq i\leq\ell
, let
W_{i}:=e_{i}\setminus V_{i-1}
.
Note that
V_{\mathcal{S}}=\bigcup_{1\leq i\leq\ell}W_{i}
and that for all
1\leq i<j\leq\ell
, we have
W_{i}\cap W_{j}=\emptyset
.
Hence,
\lvert V_{\mathcal{S}}\rvert=\sum_{1\leq i\leq\ell}\lvert W_{i}\rvert
.
Since
e_{1},\ldots,e_{\ell}
forms a
k^{\prime}
-tight self-avoiding cyclic walk, there exist distinct
k^{\prime}
-sets
U_{1},\ldots,U_{\ell}
with
U_{i}\subseteq e_{i}\cap e_{i+1}
for all
1\leq i\leq\ell
with indices taken modulo
\ell
.
Hence, for all
2\leq i\leq\ell
, we have
\lvert e_{i-1}\cap e_{i}\rvert\geq k^{\prime}
and thus
\lvert W_{i}\rvert\leq k-k^{\prime}
.
Furthermore, we have

\lvert(e_{1}\cup e_{\ell-1})\cap e_{\ell}\rvert\geq\lvert U_{\ell-1}\cup U_{\ell}\rvert\geq k^{\prime}+1

and thus
\lvert W_{\ell}\rvert\leq k-k^{\prime}-1
.
We conclude that

\rho_{\mathcal{S},e_{1}}=\frac{\ell-1}{\lparen\sum_{1\leq i\leq\ell}\lvert W_{i}\rvert\rparen-k}\geq\frac{\ell-1}{(\ell-2)(k-k^{\prime})+k-k^{\prime}-1}>\frac{\ell-1}{(\ell-1)(k-k^{\prime})}=\frac{1}{k-k^{\prime}},

which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma D.3
.

Let
1\leq k^{\prime}\leq k-1
and
\ell\leq 4
and suppose that
e_{1},\ldots,e_{\ell}
forms a
k^{\prime}
-tight self-avoiding cyclic walk.
Let
\mathcal{S}
denote the
k
-graph without isolated vertices and edge set
\mathopen{\{}e_{1},\ldots,e_{\ell}\mathclose{\}}
.
If
\mathcal{F}
is a
k^{\prime}
-cherry, then
\Phi_{\mathcal{S}}\leq n^{k-1/\rho_{\mathcal{F}}-\varepsilon^{1/7}}
.

Proof.

Suppose that
\mathcal{F}
is a
k^{\prime}
-cherry.
For
1\leq i\leq\ell
, let
\mathcal{A}_{i}
denote the
k
-graph with no isolated vertices and edge set
\mathopen{\{}e_{i},e_{i+1}\mathclose{\}}
with indices taken modulo
\ell
.
Then
\mathcal{A}_{i}
has
k
-density at least
\rho_{\mathcal{F}}
and
\mathcal{A}_{i}
is strictly
k
-balanced.
Furthermore,
\mathcal{A}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{A}_{\ell}
forms a self-avoiding cyclic walk and we have
\mathcal{S}=\mathcal{A}_{1}+\ldots+\mathcal{A}_{\ell}
.
Hence, due to Lemma
D.2
, the statement follows from Lemma
12.8
.
∎

D.2.
Overview of the argument

In this section, we show that
H(\tau_{\emptyset})\geq n^{k-1/\rho_{\mathcal{F}}-\varepsilon}
with high probability if
\mathcal{F}
is a cherry.
To this end, from now on, for this section, in addition to the setup described in Section
11
, we assume that
\mathcal{F}
is a
k^{\prime}
-cherry for some
1\leq k^{\prime}\leq k-1
and that
\mathcal{H}
is
k^{\prime}
-populated.
Furthermore, we define
i^{\star}
,
\tau^{\star}
and
V_{\mathcal{H}}
as in Section
13
.
Overall, we argue similarly as in Section
14
based on isolation, however, the structures we focus on here are different.

Still, instead of choosing the edge sets
\mathcal{F}_{0}(i)
of copies with
i\geq 1
uniformly at random in Algorithm
5.1
, we again assume that during the initialization, a linear order
\preceq
on
\mathcal{H}^{*}
is chosen uniformly at random and that for all
i\geq 1
, the edge set
\mathcal{F}_{0}(i)
is the minimum of
\mathcal{H}^{*}(i-1)
.

For a
k^{\prime}
-set
U\subseteq V_{\mathcal{H}}
and
i\geq 0
, we
use

\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{H}(i)}(U):=\mathopen{\{}e\in\mathcal{H}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}U\subseteq e\mathclose{\}}

to denote the set of edges
e\in\mathcal{H}
that contain
U
as a subset and we use

\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{H}(i)}^{*}(U):=\mathopen{\{}e\in\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{H}}(U)\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\lvert e\cap f\rvert=k^{\prime}\text{ for some }f\in\mathcal{H}\setminus\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{H}}(U)\mathclose{\}}

to denote the set of edges
e\in\mathcal{H}
that contain
U
as a subset and that are an edge of a
k^{\prime}
-cherry where not both edges contain
U
as a subset.
Note that
d_{\mathcal{H}}(U)=\lvert\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{H}}(U)\rvert
.
We set
d_{\mathcal{H}}^{*}(U):=\lvert\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{H}}^{*}(U)\rvert
.
We say that a
k^{\prime}
-set
U\subseteq V_{\mathcal{H}}
is
suitable
if there exists no sequence
e_{1},\ldots,e_{\ell}
of edges of
\mathcal{H}(0)
that forms a
k^{\prime}
-tight self-avoiding cyclic walk with
2\leq\ell\leq 4
such that
U\subseteq e_{1}
.
We use
\mathcal{U}
to denote the set of suitable
k^{\prime}
-sets.
Density considerations show that
\mathcal{U}
includes almost all
k^{\prime}
-sets
U\subseteq V_{\mathcal{H}}
.
We say that
almost-isolation
occurs at
U\in\mathcal{U}
if at some step
i\geq 0
, we have
1\leq d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}}(U)\leq 2
and
d_{\mathcal{H}}(U)\geq d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}}(U)+1
.
We say that
isolation
occurs at
U
if additionally at a later step
j>i
, we have
d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}(j)}(U)=0
while
d_{\mathcal{H}(j)}(U)
is odd hence causing at least one of the edges
e\in\mathcal{H}(j)
to eventually become an isolated vertex of
\mathcal{H}^{*}(j^{\prime})
for some
j^{\prime}\geq j
.

If at step
i=i^{\star}
, we do not already have sufficiently many edges of
\mathcal{H}
that are isolated vertices of
\mathcal{H}^{*}
, then by Lemma
13.16
, we may assume that there is essentially not more than one copy of
\mathcal{F}
for every
\lvert\mathcal{F}\rvert
edges that remain.
Hence, we are then in a situation where most of the remaining copies form a matching within
\mathcal{H}^{*}
.
We claim that for these copies that form a matching, almost-isolation must have occurred at the set
U
of vertices that both edges of the copy share if
U\in\mathcal{U}
.
This follows from Lemma
D.4
below.
Indeed, the lemma guarantees that for such
U
, there exists
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
with
d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}}(U)=1
or there exists
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}-1
with
d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}}(U)=2
,
d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}(i+1)}(U)=0
and
d_{\mathcal{H}}(U)-d_{\mathcal{H}(i+1)}(U)\geq 1
.
Almost-isolation at
U
occurs in both cases.

Lemma D.4
.

Let
U\in\mathcal{U}
and
0\leq i\leq i^{\star}
.
Then
\Delta d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}}(U):=d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}}(U)-d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}(i+1)}(U)\leq 2
.
Furthermore, if
\Delta d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}}(U)=2
, then
U\subseteq f
for all
f\in\mathcal{F}_{0}
.

Proof.

We only assume that
U\subseteq V_{\mathcal{H}}
is a
k^{\prime}
-set and show that
\Delta d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}}(U)\geq 3
entails
U\notin\mathcal{U}
and furthermore that if
\Delta d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}}(U)=2
and
U\not\subseteq f
for some
f\in\mathcal{F}_{0}
, then again
U\notin\mathcal{U}
.
We distinguish three cases.

For the first case, assume that
U\subseteq f
for all
f\in\mathcal{F}_{0}
.
Then, only the edges of
\mathcal{F}_{0}
can potentially be elements in
\Delta\mathcal{D}^{*}:=\mathcal{D}^{*}_{\mathcal{H}(i-1)}(U)\setminus\mathcal{D}^{*}_{\mathcal{H}}(U)
, so we have
\lvert\Delta\mathcal{D}^{*}\rvert\leq 2
.

For the second case, assume that there is exactly one
f\in\mathcal{F}_{0}
with
U\subseteq f
.
Then if
\lvert\Delta\mathcal{D}^{*}\rvert\geq 2
, there exists
e\in\Delta\mathcal{D}^{*}\setminus\mathcal{F}_{0}
.
For
e
to be in
\Delta\mathcal{D}^{*}
, it is necessary that there exists
f\in\mathcal{F}_{0}
with
U\not\subseteq f
and
\lvert e\cap f\rvert=k^{\prime}
.
There is only one possible choice for
f
and for this edge
f
, using
f^{\prime}
to denote the other edge in
\mathcal{F}_{0}
, if
e,f^{\prime}
does not form a
k^{\prime}
-tight self-avoiding cyclic walk, then
e,f,f^{\prime}
forms a
k^{\prime}
-tight self-avoiding cyclic walk.

For the third case, assume that
U\not\subseteq f
for all
f\in\mathcal{F}_{0}
.
Then if
\lvert\Delta\mathcal{D}^{*}\rvert\geq 2
, there exist distinct
e_{1},e_{2}\in\Delta\mathcal{D}^{*}\setminus\mathcal{F}_{0}
such that for
e\in\mathopen{\{}e_{1},e_{2}\mathclose{\}}
, there exists
f\in\mathcal{F}_{0}
with
\lvert e\cap f\rvert=k^{\prime}
.
If
e_{1},e_{2}
does not form a
k^{\prime}
-tight self-avoiding cyclic walk and if for all
f\in\mathcal{F}_{0}
, the sequence
e_{1},e_{2},f
does not form a
k^{\prime}
-tight self-avoiding cyclic walk, then, using
f
and
f^{\prime}
to denote the edges of
\mathcal{F}_{0}
, the sequence
e_{1},e_{2},f,f^{\prime}
forms a
k^{\prime}
-tight self-avoiding cyclic walk.

Furthermore, our above arguments show that if
\Delta d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}}(U)=2
and
U\not\subseteq f
for some
f\in\mathcal{F}_{0}
, then
U\notin\mathcal{U}
.
∎

Overall, our argument shows that, if eventually most of the remaining copies form a matching within
\mathcal{H}^{*}
, almost-isolation must have occurred many times.
In all cases where almost-isolation occurs, it is possible that this turns into isolation and the probability that this happens is not too small.
We ensure that the
k^{\prime}
-sets at which we look for almost isolation are spaced out as this allows us to assume that at these sets, almost-isolation turns into isolation independently of the development at the other sets.

D.3.
Formal setup

Formally, our setup is as follows.
For
\ell\geq 1
, a
k
-graph
\mathcal{A}
and a
k^{\prime}
-set
U\subseteq V_{\mathcal{A}}
, we inductively define
\mathcal{W}^{\ell}_{\mathcal{A}}(U)
as follows.
Let

\mathcal{W}^{1}_{\mathcal{A}}(U):=\mathopen{\bigg\{}U^{\prime}\in\binom{V_{\mathcal{A}}}{k^{\prime}}\mathrel{\bigg\colonDelimiter}d_{\mathcal{A}}(U\cup U^{\prime})\geq 1\mathclose{\bigg\}}

and for
\ell\geq 2
, let

\mathcal{W}^{\ell}_{\mathcal{A}}(U):=\bigcup_{U^{\prime}\in\mathcal{W}^{\ell-1}_{\mathcal{A}}(U)}\mathcal{W}^{1}_{\mathcal{A}}(U^{\prime}).

For
\ell\geq 1
, let
W^{\ell}_{\mathcal{A}}(U):=\lvert\mathcal{W}^{\ell}_{\mathcal{A}}(U)\rvert
.
Similarly as in Section
14.2
, during the random removal process, starting at step
i^{\star}
, we additionally construct random subsets
\emptyset=:\mathcal{R}(0)\subseteq\ldots\subseteq\mathcal{R}(i^{\star})\subseteq\mathcal{U}
where we collect
k^{\prime}
-sets at which almost isolation occurs.
We inductively define
\mathcal{R}(i)
with
1\leq i\leq i^{\star}
as described by the following procedure.

Algorithm D.1

Construction of
\mathcal{R}(i)
.

1

\mathcal{R}(i)\leftarrow\mathcal{R}(i-1)

2

consider an arbitrary ordering
U_{1},\ldots,U_{\ell}
of
\mathcal{U}

3

for

\ell^{\prime}\leftarrow 1

to

\ell

do

4

if

i=\min\mathopen{\{}j\geq 0\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}1\leq d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}(j)}(U_{\ell^{\prime}})\leq 2\text{ and }d_{\mathcal{H}(j)}(U_{\ell^{\prime}})\geq d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}(j)}(U_{\ell^{\prime}})+1\mathclose{\}}
and
W_{\mathcal{H}(0)}^{4}(U_{\ell^{\prime}})\cap\mathcal{R}(i)=\emptyset

then

5

\mathcal{R}(i)\leftarrow\mathcal{R}(i)\cup\mathopen{\{}U_{\ell^{\prime}}\mathclose{\}}

6

end if

7

end for

For
U\in\mathcal{R}(i^{\star})
, let
i_{U}:=\min\mathopen{\{}i\geq 0\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}U\in\mathcal{R}(i)\mathclose{\}}
.
To define events that entail almost-isolation becoming isolation, for
U\in\mathcal{R}(i^{\star})
choose possibly non-distinct copies
\mathcal{G}_{U},\mathcal{G}_{U}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{H}^{*}(i_{U})
of
\mathcal{F}
whose vertex sets contain
U
as a subset as follows.

(i)

If
d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}(i_{U})}(U)=1
and
d_{\mathcal{H}(i_{U})}(U)
is even, choose
\mathcal{G}_{U}=\mathcal{G}_{U}^{\prime}
such that one edge of
\mathcal{G}_{U}
is in
\mathcal{D}^{*}_{\mathcal{H}(i_{U})}(U)
while the other edge of
\mathcal{G}_{U}
is not in
\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{H}(i_{U})}(U)
.

(ii)

If
d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}(i_{U})}(U)=1
and
d_{\mathcal{H}(i_{U})}(U)
is odd, choose
\mathcal{G}_{U}=\mathcal{G}_{U}^{\prime}
such that one edge of
\mathcal{G}_{U}
is in
\mathcal{D}^{*}_{\mathcal{H}(i_{U})}(U)
while the other edge of
\mathcal{G}_{U}
is in
\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{H}(i_{U})}(U)
.

(iii)

If
d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}(j)}(U)=2
and
d_{\mathcal{H}(i_{U})}(U)
is even, choose
\mathcal{G}_{U}\neq\mathcal{G}_{U}^{\prime}
with
\mathcal{G}_{U}\cap\mathcal{G}_{U}^{\prime}=\emptyset
such that one edge of
\mathcal{G}_{U}
is in
\mathcal{D}^{*}_{\mathcal{H}(i_{U})}(U)
while the other edge of
\mathcal{G}_{U}
is not in
\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{H}(i_{U})}(U)
and such that one edge of
\mathcal{G}_{U}^{\prime}
is in
\mathcal{D}^{*}_{\mathcal{H}(i_{U})}(U)
while the other edge of
\mathcal{G}_{U}^{\prime}
is in
\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{H}(i_{U})}(U)

(iv)

If
d^{*}_{\mathcal{H}(j)}(U)=2
and
d_{\mathcal{H}(i_{U})}(U)
is odd, choose
\mathcal{G}_{U}=\mathcal{G}_{U}^{\prime}
such that both edges of
\mathcal{G}_{U}
are in
\mathcal{D}^{*}_{\mathcal{H}(i_{U})}
.

Let

\mathcal{E}_{U}:=\mathopen{\{}\mathcal{G}_{U}\preceq\mathcal{G}\text{ for all }\mathcal{G}\in\mathcal{N}^{1}_{\mathcal{H}^{*}(0)}(\mathcal{G}_{U})\text{ and }\mathcal{G}_{U}^{\prime}\preceq\mathcal{G}\text{ for all }\mathcal{G}\in\mathcal{N}^{1}_{\mathcal{H}^{*}(0)}(\mathcal{G}_{U}^{\prime})\mathclose{\}}.

Figure 2.
Examples for choices of the copies
\mathcal{G}_{U}
and
\mathcal{G}_{U}^{\prime}
for the special case where
\mathcal{F}
is a
3
-uniform
1
-cherry.
Each example shows the situation of the edges containing
U=\mathopen{\{}u\mathclose{\}}
as a subset at step
i_{U}
.

D.4.
Proof of Theorem
D.1

As in Section
14.3
, since every almost-isolation that turns into isolation causes an edge of
\mathcal{H}(0)
to become an isolated vertex of
\mathcal{H}^{*}
at some step
i\geq 0
and hence an edge that remains at the end of the removal process, we obtain the following statement.

Observation D.5
.

H(\tau_{\emptyset})\geq\sum_{U\in\mathcal{R}(i^{\star})}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{U}}
.

We again organize the formal presentation of the arguments outlined above into suitable lemmas.
Some of these are similar to those in Section
14.3
.
Combining the lemmas with the above observation, we then obtain Theorem
D.1
.
We define the event
\mathcal{E}_{0}
as in Section
14.3
.

Lemma D.6
.

Let
\mathcal{X}:=\mathopen{\{}i^{\star}<\tau^{\star}\mathclose{\}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{0}
.
Then,
\lvert\mathcal{R}(i^{\star})\rvert\geq_{\mathcal{X}}n^{k-1/\rho_{\mathcal{F}}-5\varepsilon^{2}}
.

Proof.

We argue similarly as in the proof of Lemma
14.2
.
Let
\mathcal{A}
denote the
k
-graph with no isolated vertices and exactly one edge and fix a
k^{\prime}
-set
I\subseteq V_{\mathcal{A}}
.
Consider a
k^{\prime}
-set
U\subseteq V_{\mathcal{H}}
and
\psi\colon I\hookrightarrow U
.
Combining the fact that
\mathcal{H}(0)
is
k^{\prime}
-populated and Lemma
12.1
, we have

(D.1)

2\leq d_{\mathcal{H}(0)}(U)\leq\Phi_{\mathcal{A},\psi}\leq n^{\varepsilon^{3}}.

Let
i:=i^{\star}
and consider the set

\mathcal{I}^{*}:=\mathopen{\{}\mathcal{F}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{H}^{*}\mathrel{\colonDelimiter}\mathcal{N}_{\mathcal{H}^{*}}^{1}(\mathcal{F}^{\prime})=\mathopen{\{}\mathcal{F}^{\prime}\mathclose{\}}\mathclose{\}}

of edge sets of copies of
\mathcal{F}
in
\mathcal{H}
that are isolated in the sense that they do not share an edge with another copy of
\mathcal{F}
.
Let
\iota\colon\mathcal{I}^{*}\to 2^{V_{\mathcal{H}}}
denote the function such that
\iota(\mathcal{F}^{\prime})
is the intersection of the two edges of
\mathcal{F}^{\prime}
for all
\mathcal{F}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{I}^{*}
.
As a consequence of the lower bound in (
D.1
), for all
U\in\mathcal{J}:=\iota(\mathcal{I}^{*})\cap\mathcal{U}
almost isolation must have occurred at
U
due to Lemma
D.4
(see discussion in the paragraph before Lemma
D.4
).
Thus, either
U
itself is an element of
\mathcal{R}
or there exists some
U^{\prime}\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathcal{H}^{*}(0)}^{4}(U^{\prime})\cap\mathcal{R}
that prevented the inclusion of
U
in
\mathcal{R}
.
Hence, we may choose a function
\pi\colon\mathcal{J}\to\mathcal{R}
that for every
U\in\mathcal{J}
chooses a witness
\pi(U)
with
\pi(U)\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathcal{H}^{*}(0)}^{4}(U)
or equivalently
U\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathcal{H}^{*}(0)}^{4}(\pi(U))
.
If
U\in\mathcal{R}
and
U^{\prime}\in\pi^{-1}(U)
, we have
U^{\prime}\in\mathcal{W}_{\mathcal{H}^{*}(0)}^{4}(U)
and hence
\pi^{-1}(U)\subseteq\mathcal{W}_{\mathcal{H}^{*}(0)}^{4}(U)
.
